- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/065-session-08-03.md`; chapter 65; scene summary-native:065:scene:003

### Session-End State

GM’s own wrap-up at the close of play (transcript ~01:35:40):

- **Bookwyrm is dead** (killed by Moziqodo, who took her key and delivered it to A'lai).

- **Moziqodo is dead** (killed by the party in one round).

- **Alkrist neutralized**—alive, but disgraced and no longer capable of defending the keep (GM ruling 2026-08-19).

- **A'lai Aivenmore captured**, bound, weeping, and in the party’s custody. He betrayed the party after surrendering but remains alive because he knows what Manshoon seeks inside the depository.

- **Kalan Strongbranch is “to the wind”**—fled, whereabouts unknown.

- **Tadric alive.** He voluntarily gave the real High Tower key to Gyrgum.

- **The Zhentarim assassin is dead** (killed by Thorin and subsequently searched), as are both thugs—one melted by Gyrgum’s acid, one killed by A'lai himself. **No surviving Zhentarim prisoner.**

- **Zalthir and Daz are conscious.** Gyrgum revived Zalthir with *healing word* and later restored forty hit points to Daz with a stronger, unnamed healing spell.

- **Manshoon’s simulacrum is inside Candlekeep**, alone and magically depleted, having shattered the security control room door after expending enormous resources to breach the keep.

- **Manshoon’s simulacrum does not know everything he needs to know.** A'lai withheld the truth about the depository and intends to trade that information for protection.

**Key/artifact ledger at session end:** Gyrgum holds the real High Tower key, the fake key, and the sapphire; crushing the sapphire recalls the real key he already holds. A'lai’s second real key is hidden inside Glabbagool. The two real keys are being deliberately kept apart.

*Exported from GMAssistant on 2026-08-09*

*Corrected 2026-08-10 against `GMT20260804-005646_Recording.transcript.cleaned.vtt`. The export had Tadric and Moziqodo swapped (Tadric alive and cooperative, Moziqodo killed by the party), omitted Bookwyrm’s death entirely, and asserted a “prophecy chanting fell silent” event that belongs to the previous session, not this one (ch64 tape 00:24:23, “The endless chant has stopped.”; on this tape it appears only inside the GM’s read-aloud recap). Moziqodo’s pronoun is **he/him** (GM ruling 2026-08-10, following `planning.md` “Sylvira Savikas’s son” / “his blood tie to Sylvira”; the export’s stray “Her death” was the error). The Zhentarim assassin was **killed, not knocked unconscious** (GM ruling 2026-08-11; corroborated at transcript L3247, “I searched the body of the assassin”).*

*Transcript L3956 (“A'lai is captured, Janussi is captured”) is a **verbal stutter, not a fact**—a duplicated clause after a false start. Janussi has been dead since ch53. **A'lai is the only prisoner.** (GM ruling 2026-08-11.)*

*Pattern worth watching: this export softened or transposed **three** combat outcomes—Tadric/Moziqodo swapped, Bookwyrm’s death dropped entirely, and a kill downgraded to unconsciousness. Verify every death claim in future gm-assist exports against the transcript before building on it.*

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/065-session-08-03.md`; chapter 65; section Session-End State

# Memorable Moments

_Exact bodies from the source summaries; no synthesis._

## Chapter 002

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/002-session-untitled.md`; chapter 2; section Memorable Moments

**Gyrgum turns forced kitchen duty into a performance, winning over the drow with an exceptional meal. When they grudgingly suggest he may not be completely worthless, he describes the dish as a mashup of giant and orc cuisine. Back in the slave pen, Shuushar praises the cooking's almost religious effect, and Gyrgum replies, “Godlike affectation is what I aspire to, sir.”**

**Gyrgum's first attempt to steal a kitchen knife goes spectacularly poorly. Caught visibly holding it, he improvises: “I'm going to sharpen this knife.” The drow let him off because of the excellent meal, but warn through Shuushar, “don't do that. Or next time, you may find yourself missing various body parts.”**

**On the following day, Topsy and Turvy steal two knives for the group. The prisoners jokingly ask whether they are magical, prompting the assessment that they are “plus zero” magical daggers with no special abilities—the D&D equivalent of “a zero ohm resistor.”**

**Buppido unveils his first escape strategy: “It's quite simple. All of you will distract the enemy while I escape through the waterfall.” After the party flatters him into revising it, he grandly announces, “since you have pledged your swords … your spells … your lives, at least, to protecting the divine plan, I will grant you insight into its mysteries later this evening.”**

**Jorlan Duskryn, his face and sword hand horribly scarred by a black pudding, approaches during his watch and asks, “if I gave you a means to escape, would you take it?” Challenged on his motives, he admits, “hate Ilvara, and I hate Shoor. And your escape, even your attempted escape, would ruin them.”**

**When the prisoners ask what they should do after jumping into the pool, Jorlan makes the limits of his assistance plain: “that is not my problem.” He wants Ilvara embarrassed; whether the prisoners survive is irrelevant to him.**

**Prince Derendil demands the dignity owed to an elven prince while hauling water into Ilvara's chambers. Ilvara responds by drawing a knife and indicating that, if he wants to be “dealt with,” she is prepared to oblige.**

**Jimjar offers to take bets despite the prisoners having no money: “As long as you're willing to trust me as the bookkeeper, I'm willing to go on credit.” The unnerving part is that he appears capable of remembering every debt perfectly.**

**Zalthir discovers a mysterious list of people from a nearby settlement among the rubbish and detritus accumulated inside the lift basket.**

**Discussing possible destinations in the Underdark, the prisoners ask whether anyone in Gracklstugh might be friendly. The grim answer is: “In the Underdark, friendly is a privilege of the dead.”**

## Chapter 003

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/003-session-untitled.md`; chapter 3; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir and Daz attempt to sneak toward the guard post but fail miserably, tripping and making enough noise to alert the guards playing cards.**

> **Zalthir:** “I rolled a one, Daz. I’m hoping you’ll do better than me.”  
> **Daz:** “Wow. I rolled a six.”  
> **Zalthir:** “That went well.”  
> **Daz:** “Plan B. Charge.”

**Thorin throws an unarmed punch at the overwhelmingly dangerous Elite Warrior, misses, and immediately realizes how badly the escape has begun.**

> **Thorin:** “My best bet is to try and sucker punch the elite warrior. … It’s high risk, high reward.”  
> **Thorin:** “Shit.”  
> **DM:** “Die rolls are not in your favor.”  
> **Thorin:** “They really aren’t.”

**Zalthir’s extraordinary streak of failed attacks prompts concern that he might be using the wrong die.**

> **Zalthir:** “I rolled two ones and a two. Game’s off to a great start.”  
> **Daz:** “There’s something wrong with the RNG.”  
> **Daz:** “Hey, Zalthir, are you rolling a D4 or a D20? Just checking.”

**Thorin, battered by Imbros, prepares a contingency plan for replacing his painstakingly created character.**

> **Thorin:** “If my guy dies, I’m going to resurrect him and do the exact same guy because I spent way too much time putting…”  
> **Thorin:** “Thorin’s sibling is going to come back, who’s an identical twin, and I’ll choose a different first name.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “Thorin’s sibling, Borin.”

**The Drow Elite Warrior focuses his fury on Prince Derendil, striking him down with a series of lethal blows from his poisoned blade.**

> **DM:** “He kills Prince Derendil.”  
> **DM:** “We barely knew you.”  
> **Thorin:** “Is Prince Derendil dead?”  
> **DM:** “He’s dead.”

**Sarith Kzekarit is kept from shouting and alerting the outpost through Gyrgum’s improvised spiritual counseling.**

> **Gyrgum:** “Think of your spirituality and how much better it would be if all this came out.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “Encourage him to meditate.”  
> **DM:** “He goes, never thought of that.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “The power of meditation.”

**Sarith, fueled by vengeance and newfound spiritual focus after being calmed by Gyrgum, delivers the killing blow to the Drow Elite Warrior.**

**Thorin survives the Drow Elite Warrior’s blows only to be knocked unconscious by a drow guard, and is brought back by Gyrgum’s divine healing in the heat of battle.**

> **Gyrgum:** “I cast cure wounds on Thorin.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “16, so you’re back up to full capacity.”

**Stool releases rapport spores, establishing a silent telepathic link among all the prisoners and enabling the coordinated escape plan.**

> **DM:** “You can all, everybody, all prisoners can now speak telepathically to each other.”  
> **Thorin:** “For an hour. Essentially forever. Six seconds at a time.”

## Chapter 004

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/004-session-untitled.md`; chapter 4; section Memorable Moments

**Topsy and Turvy reveal their wererat nature by transforming into rats and leaping down from the heights of Velkynvelve to facilitate the escape.**

> **Topsy and Turvy:** “we'll meet you downstairs”

**Sarith mixes sound tactical advice with his unstable hunger for retaliation.**

> **Sarith:** “the quicker we get out of here, the better.”

> **Sarith:** “the revenge is so tasty.”

**Ront charges a wounded vrock demon with his great axe to prove his worth, only to be struck down and killed by the demon's talons. Shuushar refuses to follow him into the doomed fight.**

> **Shuushar:** “you crazy. I not following you.”

**After Thorin shrugs off the Tongue of Madness unharmed, Daz eats it and begins compulsively blurting out every thought aloud, kicking off an hour of chaotic, involuntary honesty for the whole party.**

> **Daz:** “why are all these people laughing at me?”

**Zalthir's involuntary meditation chants pour out uncontrollably after consuming the Tongue of Madness mushroom.**

> **Zalthir:** “I'm meditating.”

**Thorin offers two pieces of bleak culinary and medical wisdom while the party experiments with Underdark fungi.**

> **Thorin:** “Every mushroom is edible once.”

> **Thorin:** “Bleeding eventually stops.”

**A sudden rockfall collapses the tunnel ceiling onto the party, knocking Daz unconscious and trapping Gyrgum, Buppido, and others under crushing rubble.**

> **GM:** “if you stay under rubble for one round you will take an additional 1d6 points of damage”

## Chapter 005

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/005-session-untitled.md`; chapter 5; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir, startled and proud, scorches the rushing carrion crawler with a blast of fire breath, sending it reeling.**

*Startled and proud after scorching a rushing carrion crawler with his breath weapon.*

**Thorin hoists the slow-moving Stool onto his back so the group can flee at full speed from the closing Drow scouts.**

*During the desperate flight from Drow scouts, Thorin ensures no one is left behind.*
- Thorin: "All right. We're doing this Yoda style. Let's go."

**Stool releases telepathic rapport spores, allowing the entire party to hold a silent, private conversation in their minds while Drow scouts lurk nearby.**

*During the chase, the party needed to talk without alerting the Drow scouts on their trail.*
- GM: "Okay, so you're now all chattering in your head. Was that a good idea? I thought it was a good idea. I'm really glad you all liked my good idea."

**Gyrgum notices Sarith's navigational error and corrects their course, saving the group from getting lost at a critical moment.**

*Sarith's pride nearly led the group astray while fleeing at a fast pace through the Faerzress tunnels.*
- Sarith: "Yes, I saw that. Thank you. I didn't need your help. But it's good we're all on the same page."
- Gyrgum: "I'm wise like that."
- Sarith: "Yes. You are very wise." (paraphrase of the GM's "He goes, yes. You are very wise.")

**Stool checks on Gyrgum after the rockfall.**
- Gyrgum: "Oh, I'm not doing so well. I got pretty badly hurt."

**Dwarves stick together.**
- Thorin, choosing whom the companions should rescue: "Save Eldeth because, you know, dwarves stick together."
- Daz's player, out of character: "Let's save the other people. If we get hit, maybe they'll see that we're saving others."

**The carrion crawler tastes like chicken.**
- GM: "It's tasty." ... "Taste like chicken."
- Gyrgum: "I've eaten some delicious adventures."

**Thorin on running from pursuers.**
- "You don't have to be faster than the bear. You just have to be faster than anybody you're going with."

**Jimjar's thwarted wager.**
- Jimjar: "I was going to make a bet that I was going to find food and you guys weren't. But at the pace that the silent one wants us to go today, I don't think anybody of us is going to find any food."

## Chapter 006

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/006-session-untitled.md`; chapter 6; section Memorable Moments

**Jimjar bets against himself on whether he can disguise the tunnel blockage, disguises it anyway, and ends up owing Gyrgum five gold pieces — revealing he bets purely for the thrill, not to win.**

*The party slowly realizes Jimjar's gambling obsession has nothing to do with money and everything to do with the act of betting itself*

> "Look, and if we never have money, you never have to pay. Think of it that way."
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's pitch for why a penniless party should take his bet anyway*

> "I keep track of all of my debts and victories."
> — Jimjar (as narrated by the GM)

*Jimjar makes clear that every wager, won or lost, goes into his ledger*

> "I like your enthusiasm, Zalthir, but … can I do something stupid? Anybody can not do something stupid. The question is, when there's an important action to perform, can I do it?"
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar turns down Zalthir's bet that he can't fly to the roof of the cave, showing there is a method to his madness*

> "Wait, so this is interesting. He bet against himself, knowing full well the odds were well against him, implying that he'll make awful offers for bets in the future."
> — Zalthir

*The moment the party figures out Jimjar's pattern*

> "He goes, no, but it would mean that we looked better. Didn't you want to be found looking your best?"
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's answer when asked whether glowing Ormu paint would help them hide*

> "Hi, my name is Yuk Yuk."
> "And mine is Spiderbait."
> — the goblin guides

*The goblins' introduction at the edge of the Silken Paths*

> "I'll make you a bet that you don't know how to travel this."
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's opening move with the goblins, which leads to their demonstration run across the webs*

> "Did you see me nail those tough strings? I mean, there was this one moment when I was taking a curve and the strands broke, but I managed to do that twist and flip. And like, that was just like super cool."
> — Yuk Yuk

*The goblin surfer-dude act in full swing after their demonstration*

> "Oh, yeah, you're not supposed to do that sliding shit. That's advanced techniques. We'll just walk."
> — the goblin guides

*Reassuring the party that they will not be expected to web-surf*

> "I'm getting more anxious with every sentence. Maybe we should just go."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's reaction to the goblins explaining how many spiders live in the chasm*

> "I want to go web surfing across the underdark."
> — one of the goblin guides

*One of the goblins cheerfully singing to himself as the party prepares to cross the terrifying Silken Paths*

> "You remember that time Bobby got eaten by a spider?"
> "Dude, why are you bringing us down, man?"
> — the goblin guides

*One of the guides brings up a friend eaten by a spider, and the other shoots it down for killing the vibe*

**Daz spots the twins Topsy and Turvy whispering secretly to each other in the dark before sleep, but cannot make out what they are saying or discern their intentions.**

*A quiet, unsettling moment at camp that raises questions about the twins' loyalty and what they may be planning*

> "I'm really helpful, aren't I? … Can I come on the mission or do I have to stay behind?"
> — Stool

*Stool's eagerness as he sends his spores toward the moving cocoon*

> "Anybody with that ridiculous of a name? Could they be evil?"
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's first read on Fargas Rumblefoot*

> "I can assure you, I was not infected."
> "That's what I used to say in the zombie apocalypse."
> — Fargas Rumblefoot and Thorin

*Thorin is unconvinced by the cocooned halfling's reassurances*

> "Dudes, I have a really bad feeling about this, but I think there are some spiders that are coming here. If we want to do anything, we better do it now and run like crazy or something."
> — Yuk Yuk

*The warning that turns the cocoon debate into a race against time*

**Fargas Rumblefoot, still cocooned and with giant spiders closing in, desperately bargains for his life by promising to lead the party to a lost tomb filled with treasure.**

> "I know the location of the tomb, and more importantly, I know the location of the treasure."
> — Fargas Rumblefoot

*A tense standoff as the party weighs the risk of stopping to free a stranger against the lure of hidden treasure*

**Thorin slashes through the thick spider web cocoon in a single decisive strike, freeing Fargas Rumblefoot just as giant spiders begin closing in on the party's position.**

> "It's the first good roll I've gotten since we started playing."
> — Thorin

*A clutch rescue under pressure, with the party scrambling to flee across the webs immediately after*

## Chapter 007

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/007-session-untitled.md`; chapter 7; section Memorable Moments

> "Fargas, I remember you said, and I quote, I have information. I know the location of the tomb. And more importantly, I know the location of the treasure."
> — Gyrgum

> "Ah, did I say that?"
> — Fargas

> "Ah, you did. It's written down right here."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum confronted Fargas about his earlier boast of knowing the tomb's location, producing his notes as evidence while Fargas tried to pretend he had never said anything. Fargas studied the notes and conceded: "yes, apparently I did say this."*

> "Is it full of Brys?"
> — Thorin

*Thorin's response to Fargas's revelation about the tomb of the sorcerer Brysis. He misheard the name, which prompted Fargas to insist on the correct pronunciation. Fargas then complimented the corrected version as "a very good pronunciation," leaving the party unsure whether he was mocking Dragonborn and their excessive use of the letter S.*

> "I thought you said you knew where the treasure was."
> — Daz

*Daz, after Fargas admitted he did not know the tomb's precise location.*

**Thorin severs a web strand with a single powerful sword stroke for nine damage, sending a giant spider plummeting into the darkness below.**

*A decisive early move in the spider encounter that bought the party crucial time to escape the Silken Paths.*

**Daz readies his magical strike, waiting for his allies to clear a web intersection, then blasts it apart with all three magic missile darts. The blast severs two strands at once and cuts off the spiders' pursuit.**

*A carefully coordinated tactical maneuver that turned the tide of the spider chase and allowed the party to escape.*

> "Picked the wrong day to stop sniffing glue."
> — Thorin

*Thorin's quip during the scramble off the webs.*

> "That was way more exciting than I thought it would be."
> — DM

*The DM, after the last party member disengaged and fled south ahead of five giant spiders.*

> "No, no, man. This isn't good, man. This is a beholder. Well, I mean, not a real beholder, but still damn scary."
> — Yuk Yuk and Spiderbait (voiced by the DM)

*The goblin guides' warning as the spectator appeared.*

> "I bring warnings."
> — Spectator

> "You are all demons. Yes, you are a demon pack."
> — Spectator

> "Now, we're not a demon pack."
> — Daz

> "I'll bet him a gold piece that he can't get this guy not to attack us."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum baited the wager-loving Jimjar into trying to talk down the spectator. The roll came up a two.*

> "The demons are coming. The demons are coming."
> — Spectator

*The dying words of the paranoid spectator, a cryptic warning delivered telepathically as it faded from existence after the party slew it.*

**The party attempts to use telepathic mushroom spores to communicate with a suspicious chest, hoping to determine whether it is a mimic. They get no response and quietly walk around it.**

*A creative but ultimately fruitless idea. The DM called it "brilliant" and noted that no one had ever tried it in all his years of campaigning.*

> "I mean, it's not like I've seen spiders show up in the middle of the night and eat other groups."
> — Yuk Yuk (voiced by the DM)

*The guide's reassurance when Daz proposed a long rest beside the chest. It convinced no one.*

> "I'm good for it, guys. All you have to do is follow me until we find some way to get you the money you need."
> — Jimjar

> "I mean, if we're feeling evil, we could also suggest they retrieve the gold from the chest."
> — Zalthir

> "Perhaps we wish to discuss how you too can be part of my divine plan."
> — Buppido

*Buppido, the unsettling derro companion, ominously invited the party to join his mysterious and clearly sinister divine plan after they joked about sending the goblins to loot a dangerous chest.*

> "Maybe he's the demon."
> — Zalthir

## Chapter 008

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/008-session-untitled.md`; chapter 8; section Memorable Moments

> "We were told we wouldn't have to do anything."
> — Gyrgum

> "What? You're just going to show up here and die? Is that your plan?"
> — Hemeth

*Hemeth's horrified reply when he realized the party wasn't planning to resist.*

> "I'm an arms dealer, and I was just trying to close a deal with one of the two factions, but apparently I picked the wrong faction to make a deal with."
> — Hemeth

> "I'm just a neutral arms dealer. I should not be being used."
> — Hemeth

> "I accept your offerings in the name of the deep father. May their blood nourish and strengthen him."
> — Bloppblippodd

*Spoken as she accepted the party as sacrifices. A burbling cheer went up from the surrounding Kuo-toa.*

> "does anybody want to bet on how many of us are going to come out of this alive?"
> — Jimjar

> "We're all going to come out alive."
> — Daz

> "I'll take the other side of that bet."
> — Jimjar

*Responding to Daz's confident claim that everyone would survive the ritual sacrifice. Jimjar stayed true to his gambling nature even facing death.*

> "It'd be cheating for him to actually kill one of us to win his bet."
> — Gyrgum

> "Tell me when it's my time to act. And if we get out of this alive, look me up in Gracklstugh."
> — Hemeth

*Whispered desperately to Gyrgum while awaiting sacrifice at the Kuo-toan altar, a bedraggled arms dealer clinging to any hope of survival.*

> "Bring me the knife!"
> — Bloppblippodd

**Just as Bloppblippodd calls for the sacrificial knife, Ploopploopeen strikes his own daughter with his scepters, triggering a full-scale Kuo-toan civil war.**

*The shocking betrayal that turned a ritual sacrifice into a chaotic melee and freed the party from their immediate fate.*

> "well, I'm surprised we're still all alive, but it's still soon. Still too soon to tell."
> — Jimjar

> "Well, I don't know if I'd call it stealing. I mean, he promised us stuff for helping, and we've helped."
> — Gyrgum

*Justifying the raid on Ploopploopeen's hut.*

> "We're like the reservoir dogs. That's the walk that's happening."
> — Daz

*Describing the party's confident, un-rushed stroll toward the boats.*

**Pools of blood and churning water appear across the Darklake as Kuo-toans are dragged beneath the surface by an unseen predator.**

*Thorin notices the ominous signs while the party flees toward the docks, foreshadowing the horror about to emerge.*

> "So, okay, that actually makes me wonder whether the whole plan for the sacrifice was to put us in a boat and just capsize it."
> — Gyrgum

> "I mean, so the floor is lava."
> — Gyrgum

**Topsy and Turvy transform into rats and frantically scurry toward Bloppblippodd's lair to search for treasure while everyone else is fleeing for their lives.**

*In a life-or-death escape from a Kuo-toan civil war, the twins' priority was loot rather than survival.*

> "I don't want to die. I don't want to die."
> — Spiderbait

> "Leemooggoogoon!"
> — Bloppblippodd

*The dying cry of the archpriest as her father struck her down before the altar. The invocation seemed to summon something far worse from the depths.*

**Demogorgon, a thirty-foot-tall two-headed demon lord, erupts from the Darklake in a cascade of oily tentacles and roars, ending the session on a catastrophic cliffhanger.**

*The party had just begun their escape when the Prince of Demons himself rose from the water, dwarfing every threat they had faced.*

> "I'm hearing the freaking stranger things like, you know, theme music in my head."
> — Thorin

> "We're in the underdark, but we could just as easily be in the upside down."
> — Daz

> "So, so Demogorgon is a very dangerous and powerful creature, and the most useful thing to be is anywhere but here."
> — GM

> "This confirms runaway."
> — Daz

## Chapter 009

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/009-session-untitled.md`; chapter 9; section Memorable Moments

**Demogorgon erupts from the Darklake, his two heads roaring and tentacles smashing the surface as he wades toward Sloobludop, leaving pools of blood in his wake.**
- GM: "Demogorgon is wading towards Sloobludop. His head's roaring, tentacles flailing and smashing the water."
- Daz: "As a famous wizard once said, fly, you fools."
- Thorin, on the demon-fish in the bloody water: "as they say in Apocalypse Now, never get out of the boat."

**Daz is driven into hysterics by the sight of the Prince of Demons.**
- GM: "You become incapacitated and spend the next three rounds screaming, laughing, or weeping. So your group is going to have to carry you." / "Sorry, three minutes, not three rounds."

**Gyrgum successfully persuades a group of kuo-toa to act heroically and distract the demonic forces, buying the party precious time to escape.**
- Thorin, passing the job along: "Not me. I have a charisma for shit."
- GM: "They were predisposed to be heroic, plus they are terrified of the water and the fishes in it."
- Gyrgum: "I think a lot of people, you can convince them they're natural heroes."

**Shuushar insists his people summoned Leemooggoogoon, until an escapee slaps him and names the truth: Demogorgon.**
- Shuushar (via GM): "yes, this is why we have to arrive at a better understanding of the universe. And we have to achieve enlightenment because we summoned, we summoned Leemooggoogoon."
- "Demogorgon is in the Prime Material Plane. That's not possible." / Thorin: "I don't care what's possible. I saw what I saw."
- GM: "Summoning the Prince of Demons is not something a fifth-level magic user does by reading a scroll."

**Zalthir conjures a sphere of absolute magical darkness over the Ixitxachitl predators and uses his unique sight to strike down one of the beasts while his companions struggle in the water.**
- Zalthir: "As my class skill, my subclass skill, I can see through my magical darkness."
- Gyrgum: "And an evil, invisible manta ray is even creepier than an evil visible one."
- Once everyone is back aboard: "Never get out of the boat." / "We're going to need a bigger boat."

**Hemeth's verdict on kuo-toa diplomacy.**
- Hemeth: "Last time I talked to kuo-toa, I found myself wrapped up in ropes and about to be hanged."

**Shuushar bids an emotional, sticky farewell to the party and offers a final lesson in mindfulness to an exasperated Buppido before departing with the kuo-toa refugees.**
- Shuushar: "Now that we have seen the folly of our world, it is time for a new kuo-toa society to emerge. One that is awakened from its madness."
- Shuushar: "Thank you for helping me find my calling."
- Gyrgum's response to the mindfulness lecture: "I spit."

## Chapter 010

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/010-session-untitled.md`; chapter 10; section Memorable Moments

**After sessions of joking about its uselessness, Daz finally uses Shape Water for something practical: pushing the boat off a sandbar, to the cheers of the entire party.**

> "Finally, Shape Water is going to do something."
> — Daz

> "I finally got to use it. Did you put the sandbar in just for me?"
> — Daz

> "No, but I wish I had."
> — GM

*Jimjar quipped he should have bet on whether Daz would ever use Shape Water for something useful, capping a long-running joke.*

> "I should have made the bet whether he's ever going to use shape water for something useful."
> — Jimjar

**Thorin navigates the Darklake flawlessly and credits his method to intuition.**

> "I go, like, yeah, I'm just like following the vibes. Just like I code."
> — Thorin

*Sarith had just asked, "huh? You know where you're going."*

**Daz sprays water around the suspicious abandoned keelboat, revealing an invisible duergar lurker who immediately panics and dives into the dark lake.**

*The party was wary of an ambush; the water spray confirmed their fears and kicked off the combat encounter.*

> "I love cantrips because they don't cost anything."
> — Daz

> "So there's somebody in the boat and now they're in the water and they're invisible. … I'm not sure that's better. Sorry, guys."
> — Daz

**Hemeth demonstrates duergar invisibility mid-conversation.**

> "You know the duergar can become invisible, right?"
> — Hemeth

*He then proceeded to become invisible, and whispered the rest of his explanation into Thorin's ear so enemies couldn't target him by sound.*

> "Are you deaf? Like the rest of your kind?"
> — Hemeth

*After Thorin misheard "keelboat" as "kill boat."*

> "You can arrive as a visitor or as merchandise. It's totally up to you."
> — GM as Narrator

*After the party noted they were trying to reach Gracklstugh, the DM pointed out that the duergar were slavers who could deliver them there in chains.*

> "They like to go around kidnapping people and selling them for slaves in the city of Gracklstugh."
> — Jimjar

**Buppido, the derro, lies flat on the deck during the entire battle pretending to be invisible, insisting it is all part of the 'divine plan' while his allies fight for their lives.**

*The party repeatedly glanced at Buppido in disbelief as he contributed nothing to the fight, hiding in plain sight.*

> "This whole invisibility thing could have helped when we were trying to escape, Buppido."
> — Zalthir

> "Because the divine plan said I shouldn't do it."
> — Buppido

> "He's lying here and he thinks no one can see him. So he's like a three-year-old."
> — Daz

**Zalthir moves his sphere of magical darkness across the battlefield at will, striking down enemies with precision while his foes flail helplessly.**

*The combination of Zalthir's darkness and Thorin's ten-foot blindsight proved devastatingly effective, controlling the entire combat encounter.*

> "The combo of him having that plus me having blindsight, that's going to be sweet."
> — Thorin

**Eldeth's anti-Duergar sentiments, as channeled by the GM, and Gyrgum's reading of what lies beneath them.**

> "…a demonic spawn of dwarven corruption who's a slave trader and deserves to be sent to the nine hells as punishment for her. This could go on for some time."
> — GM, imagining Eldeth's commentary

*Gyrgum's religion check suggested the hatred is political as much as religious: dwarves and duergar compete over similar resources, and Eldeth belongs to the ruling class.*

*Zalthir, on hearing that Eldeth belongs to the ruling class — to Thorin, who had admitted he has "kind of got a thing for Eldeth":*

> "You know, it's a I don't know, Thorin. Your taste is interesting."
> — Zalthir

> "If you need someone to do questioning, I am quite the expert. … I have many techniques."
> — Buppido

> "I have no doubt."
> — Thorin

**Mayonnaise over poison.**

> "I mean, we could have taken the acid or the poison, but instead we're like, nay."
> — Thorin

> "No, but we make our weapons more delicious."
> — Thorin

> "What if we made one with a fungus cake, mayonnaise, fungus cake? It might actually be good."
> — Daz

> "We're going to waterboard him with mayonnaise."
> — Thorin

*After capturing the last duergar and finding the alchemy jug, the party immediately connected their new mayonnaise supply to their interrogation plans.*

> "My best friend died. I got attacked by a demon lord."
> — Spiderbait

*Spiderbait's bleak response when the party tried to convince him to stay with them after he was nearly kidnapped by duergar slavers. Thorin's reply: "I mean, it sounds like an adventure."*

## Chapter 011

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/011-session-untitled.md`; chapter 11; section Memorable Moments

> "We are not kuo-toa with our boops and beeps and baba boots. They are mad and crazy."
> — Brannum Redmarch

*Brannum's first words to the party, offended at being lumped in with the kuo-toa.*

> "I did not realize that Thorin too was a master"
> — Sarith

*Sarith, visibly impressed, reacts to Thorin's cold and direct intimidation of their duergar prisoner, Brannum Redmarch.*

> "I like your approach direct to the point putting them on the spot but don't you think we should do some torturing just just just for you know to make sure"
> — Sarith

*Sarith, a little disappointed, suggests torture after Thorin's blunt ultimatum to Brannum Redmarch proves effective without it.*

> "if he tries to like screw us over he's the first to go"
> — Thorin

*Gyrgum proposes bringing Brannum along "as like almost a hostage," and Thorin sets the terms.*

> "I know a guy." / "That guy?" / "No, it's the other guy." / "Oh, that guy."
> — Hemeth and Buppido

*The companions eagerly workshop Thorin's idea of selling Brannum into slavery. The DM notes that "some of the people you've collected are the best."*

> "Bone Machu Picchu." / "Yes, Bone Machu Picchu. … Machu Bono."
> — Joe (Thorin's player) and the DM, out of character

*The table's name for the bone pyramid on the islet.*

> "Shit, dock! Do something! … This was a mistake. I thought we were going the other way."
> — Brannum Redmarch

*Brannum panics at the sight of the ram-skulled bone pyramid, moments before the lightning strike.*

**Gyrgum attempts to command the beholder zombie to flee, only to realize too late that the undead horror cannot understand a word he is saying.**

> "I didn't know it needed to understand my language. Oops. That's something to keep in mind."
> — Gyrgum

*A tense opening to the beholder zombie fight, as Gyrgum's first move is wasted on a creature that doesn't understand Common.*

> "Casts a ray of light emerges, sort of like, you know, straight out of Doctor Who with the Daleks." / "Exterminate."
> — The DM and Daz

*The beholder zombie's ray hits Gyrgum for 10 damage and poisons him.*

**Zalthir lands a critical strike on the beholder zombie with his bare fists, dealing a massive burst of damage that turns the tide of the fight.**

*A highlight of the beholder zombie battle, with Zalthir's unarmed critical hit dealing 17 points of damage in a single blow.*

> "I thought that that crew had just decided to abandon us. … If they came here and bled, that's one thing. But if they're dead, where are they?" / "I mean, there is a pile of bones." / "Yeah, but there's no boat."
> — Brannum Redmarch and Thorin

*Brannum's confession, just before Daz looks into the water.*

**Daz peers into the dark water and discovers the sunken remains of Brannum's lost crew: their boats on the lakebed, their bodies now walking as zombies.**

*A grim revelation that confirms Brannum's crew did not desert him, but were killed and reanimated by the horrors lurking near the bone pyramid.*

> "I mean, I'm going to regret this, but, you know, somebody's got to do it." / "Why does everybody assume that bad things are going to happen?" / "It's a chest amongst a bunch of zombies."
> — Thorin and the DM

*Thorin volunteers for the dive, and the DM protests the party's suspicion.*

**Thorin dives into the zombie-infested Darklake, protected by a shield of faith and hidden by magical darkness, and retrieves the sunken chest without alerting a single undead creature.**

*A tense and clever team effort combining Gyrgum's protective magic and Zalthir's darkness to pull off a flawless underwater heist.*

> "I should get a share of that treasure. After all, it is from my crew." … "Possibly not the best suggestion I've made this today."
> — Brannum Redmarch

> "I mean, you can go down and talk about whether or not you should get it with the zombies that are in the water if you want."
> — Daz

*Daz then twinkles his fingers and sends water spraying up around the annoyed duergar.*

> "You don't need that one. I need that one. Give me that one for food."
> — Aquatic Troll

> "I'm neutral good here. I don't think I can be tossing people to the trolls."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's answer when Daz suggests handing Brannum to the troll.*

> "Brave Sir Robin ran away."
> — Gyrgum

*The party decides to flee the troll rather than fight it.*

> "Your stomach flips as you double over, moaning first in pain, then in incantation. … And just as you are sure the spell will burn you to nothing, you straighten and vomit forth a wave of acid." / "Gyrgum's gross."
> — The DM

*The DM reads out the casting description of Tasha's Caustic Brew.*

> "I wasn't trying to take, I wasn't trying to kill you. I was trying to be helpful." / "I appreciate your perspective. It's very unique."
> — Aquatic Troll and Thorin

*The aquatic troll, retreating into the depths after being blasted with magic, insists it was only offering to reduce the number of mouths the party had to feed. Thorin, who speaks Giant, replies.*

> "The guards guard the doors, not the docks. … So just remember, this is not the time or place for heroics."
> — Hemeth

> "Oh, that is ruthless."
> — The DM, when Thorin asks whether they can ransom their prisoner

> "I mean, we didn't promise that we would let him go. We just said his only path to not dying is to not screw us over."
> — Thorin

## Chapter 012

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/012-session-untitled.md`; chapter 12; section Memorable Moments

> "I promise you the handaxe will be easily reused by the person who kills you."
> — Hemeth

*Hemeth, the duergar arms dealer, pitching the durability of his weapons to the party with a backhanded compliment about their life expectancy. Gyrgum shot back "It will barely outlast the warranty" and "Lifetime guarantee," and Hemeth added: "Yours will probably be short and brutal, but that's okay."*

> "Do you know that you saved my life? I owe you at least a 2% discount."
> — Hemeth

*Hemeth, offended that the party was bargaining with Brannum, declares the full extent of his gratitude.*

**Buppido, fed a Tongue of Madness mushroom, unleashes a five-day backlog of petty insults against every single member of the party in one long, unbroken stream of consciousness.**

*The party pressed Buppido about his plans, and he took the opportunity to air every grievance he had accumulated during their journey together. The GM relayed the highlights: "Daz has foul breath. Thorin's muscles are overrated. Eldeth is never going to go for him. Zalthir really could deal with some cleaning of his scales. … Gyrgum's petulance is beyond irritating. Jimjar needs to give it a rest with all of the betting." Zalthir's response: "I'm liking this guy more, more and more."*

> "Oh, well, I can tell you right now, we're not naming the party the fellowship."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's verdict on the party's cohesion after Buppido's tirade.*

> "I should have bet on whether he was going to say that."
> — Jimjar

*After Buppido screamed at Jimjar to stop making bets, Jimjar immediately responded by wishing he had bet on that exact outburst.*

> "obviously you don't understand the first thing about interplanar travel, gate travel, and the rituals required to summon a demon of Demogorgon's power."
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar dismissing the idea that Buppido could summon another horror. He added, "There are a great many things Buppido can do, but summoning Demogorgon is not on the list of them," then paused to wonder what could summon Demogorgon and offered to take bets on it.*

> "The neutral good cleric confronts the cruelty of the rest of the party and the abject horror of the Underdark."
> — GM

*Narrating Gyrgum's discomfort with killing Buppido, followed by: "Everybody stares at him and goes, you're new to the Underdark, aren't you?"*

> "Sounds like a great place to frame our enemies."
> — Daz

*Daz's response to learning that any duergar can execute a thief on the spot.*

**A duergar merchant turns completely invisible in the middle of a transaction but continues speaking as if nothing has happened, one of several signs of creeping madness observed among the city's inhabitants.**

*Gyrgum noticed the oddities while shopping in the Blade Bazaar. The scroll merchant who threatened him mid-haggle was the first. When the other merchant asked his non-existent twin for an opinion, a bystander explained: "Oh, no, there's no twin brother. He's an only child."*

**A two-headed stone giant bursts through the city gates in a frenzy of madness, hurling a duergar soldier's broken body near the party's feet before charging directly at them.**

*The party had just been browsing the marketplace when the giant's roar and the sound of crumbling rock announced the sudden, violent encounter.*

**Zalthir conjures a sphere of magical darkness around the rampaging stone giant, blinding the massive creature and giving the party advantage on their attacks.**

*Zalthir rushed into melee range of the enormous, madness-driven giant to deploy the darkness, a bold and risky move. The table admired the GM's new sparkly darkness effect on the map ("Oh, wow. It sparkles." / "Yes, it's sparkly.").*

> "You know, I just, you know, life's too short. You know, all I ever wanted was to come here. And we're here. And the divine plan requires that we go our separate ways. And let me give you my blessing."
> — Buppido

*Buppido, sensing the party was not going to kill him, suddenly became extremely accommodating and philosophical in a desperate bid for his freedom. Gyrgum: "Ah, yes. Empathy, my great weakness."*

## Chapter 013

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/013-session-untitled.md`; chapter 13; section Memorable Moments

> "Well, we do have some kindling here if you're short on dragons."
> — Gyrgum

*Deadpan quip to Gartokkar Xundorn after learning the Keepers of the Flame tend a dragon to light the city's forges*

**Gartokkar Xundorn laughs at Gyrgum's kindling joke, then analytically dissects its comedic structure in exhaustive detail, draining it of all humor.**

> "That was a joke. ... That was a very well-done joke. I haven't heard such a joke in a long time."
> — Gartokkar Xundorn

> "Well, you see, the key to a good joke is that you have a sudden change in perspective."
> — Gyrgum

*The literal-minded duergar cleric appreciated the joke intellectually but had no idea he was killing it by explaining it. Gyrgum helpfully joined the analysis.*

> "So he's a lighter?"
> — Daz

*Daz's summary of Themberchaud's role in the city, which the GM confirmed with an enthusiastic "Yes!"*

**Thorin urges the party and the duergar militia to spare the maddened stone giant's life, but his check to sway the militia fails badly, and the duergar cut Rihuud down anyway.**

*A tense moment where the right call was made, but the dice and the militia's rigid code ensured it didn't matter*

> "On their heads, be it."
> — Thorin

> "The crazy person, as you chose to refer to him, the person who is engaging in antisocial behaviors with no productive outcome possible, as we prefer to refer to them, is now dead."
> — the duergar militia

> "We have conferred and have concluded that, yes, there have been significantly more incidents of chaotic behavior recently."
> — the duergar militia

*The militia had to sit down and confer before answering, because Gyrgum's question asked whether there had been "more."*

**Dorhun reveals that Rihuud sprouted a second head and went mad while communing with the stone in Cairngorm Cavern, hinting at a deeper supernatural corruption spreading through Gracklstugh.**

*The party connects this to the wave of madness affecting merchants and the Deepking himself*

> "I appreciate your attempts to save Rihuud's life. He was a good man. He was a good giant."
> — Dorhun

> "Qualified immunity and all that."
> — Thorin

*Thorin's response after the militia insisted they had acted "well within our rights under the current penal code"*

> "It's not really paranoia when there are invisible people trying to get you."
> — Gyrgum

> "You are actually the safest thing I've seen in the last week. You at least won't turn invisible and start accusing me of crimes."
> — the halfling informant

> "I have not learned invisibility yet."
> — Gyrgum

> "The Deepking has gone mad."
> — the halfling informant

*A halfling informant whispers this revelation to the party outside the Darklake Brewery, lowering his voice despite being three feet tall*

**The halfling informant crouches down to whisper conspiratorially, then realizes Gyrgum towers over him, leading to an awkward renegotiation of how to have a quiet conversation.**

*A moment of physical comedy as the halfling's dramatic instincts clashed with the reality of talking to a much taller character. Gyrgum: "I stand on my tiptoes, you know."*

> "I've noticed that none of them have gone mad enough to lower the prices."
> — Gyrgum
>
> "Well, that would be beyond mad."
> — the halfling informant

> "Do I have to explain to you about the birds and the bees and eggs?"
> — the halfling informant

*The halfling's answer to how the Keepers replace a dragon, which set the drow behind the party spluttering with laughter*

> "At least there we did it with class, with style. ... There's no drama, no finesse, no art, no style. It's just violence for the sake of violence."
> — the drow patron

> "Oh come now if I wanted to have you arrested I wouldn't show up with just four of my guards I'd show up with a whole platoon"
> — Errde Blackskull

**Captain Errde Blackskull casually reveals she already knows the drow are hunting the party, offering her protection as a bargaining chip and making clear the party is far less anonymous in Gracklstugh than they thought.**

> "I can assure you that any Drow who are looking for you will not be able to find you."
> — Errde Blackskull

*A chilling reminder that in a city of invisible spies, the party's secrets are not their own. Thorin: "So we're not as sneaky as we think we are."*

## Chapter 014

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/014-session-untitled.md`; chapter 14; section Memorable Moments

**After slaying the assassins, the party eagerly reaches for the glowing blades, only to watch them vanish into thin air. They were psionic constructs that ceased to exist with their wielders.**

> "Ooh, somebody better grab that glowing blade."
> — Gyrgum

> "God damn it. Come on. It's a psychic blade. This is bullshit, man."
> — Thorin

*Collective disappointment after the party fought specifically hoping to loot the cool glowing weapons. Thorin had declared beforehand, "I want a glowing weapon."*

> "Based on what Thorin was saying, we better kill them while the blades are lit up."
> — Gyrgum

> "So I think that's the name of our group, Firefly Tactics."
> — Gyrgum

*The party names itself after an out-of-game story from Thorin's player about squashing lit fireflies as a kid in the Midwest to make their glow last. The GM approved: "I could live with that."*

> "I was standing on the corner of my own business, and then I woke up in a jail with a bunch of Drow and these Yahoos. But, you know, we seem to get along okay."
> — Thorin

*Thorin's deadpan answer when Themberchaud asked how the party had ended up in the Underdark*

> "You have no family or associations. Fantastic. Nobody who can black, nobody to be blackmailed. That's wonderful."
> — Themberchaud

*The dragon's delighted response to Thorin's rootless backstory*

> "Next! You!"
> — Themberchaud

*The arrogant red dragon's dismissive reaction upon learning Gyrgum serves Bahamut, his divine rival, immediately moving on to interrogate the next party member*

> "You didn't say I was having surfacers for dinner tonight."
> — Themberchaud

*The dragon's opening line as he lumbers into view, chuckling at his own wit*

**Themberchaud breathes fire in all directions upon entering his private chamber with the party, ensuring no invisible spies are lurking, before settling onto his mountain of gold like a caged predator.**

> "Anyone who's invisible will be turned to ash."
> — Themberchaud

*The dragon's paranoid display of power set an ominous tone for the private audience that followed. The GM described him as giving "very much a Jabba the Hutt kind of vibe."*

**The party discovers a fish-skin parchment on one of the assassins bearing a charcoal likeness of the merchant Werz, confirming the attack was a deliberate, pre-planned hit.**

*Found while looting the bodies before pushing them off the pier, raising questions about who ordered the assassination*

**Gartokkar reveals that the Keepers of the Flame are secretly grooming a replacement for Themberchaud using a stolen dragon egg, and that the dragon himself has no idea.**

> "Themberchaud thinks that we work for him. What he doesn't realize is he works for the duergar."
> — Gartokkar

*The party now holds explosive information that could destabilize the entire power structure of Gracklstugh if revealed to the dragon*

> "I'm just thinking, like, could we escape in a blaze of glory on the back of a dragon?"
> — Thorin

> "You know what? That could be an option that I did not account for."
> — GM

*Thorin pitches an out-of-the-box exit plan that catches the GM off guard*

> "If we find the egg, we'll decide what to do then."
> — Thorin

> "You are a funny person."
> — Gartokkar

> "I am short."
> — Thorin

> "The duergar are also short."
> — Gartokkar

> "So I am average."
> — Thorin

> "At least you're not freakishly tall like the elves, the orcs, and the dragonborn."
> — Gartokkar

*Thorin's blunt refusal to commit to returning the egg to the Keepers, delivered directly to Gartokkar's face after being threatened with arrest, and the height banter that followed*

**The party finds a box of scented candles among the gear of the fearsome psionic assassins, prompting jokes about the killers needing to unwind with a relaxing bath and whale sounds.**

> "I need a bath with some scented candles and some whale sounds."
> — Thorin

> "Yeah, I have free cantrips. I can make a minor illusion of your whale sounds right now."
> — Zalthir

*A moment of absurd levity immediately after a tense combat, with Zalthir offering to cast Minor Illusion for the whale sounds*

> "It's hard for me to not giggle a little when someone introduces themselves as Hulg."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's reaction to Grimholl Forgebrand's nickname*

> "I am now a keeper of the flame."
> — Daz

*Daz, after finishing the last assassin with Scorching Ray*

## Chapter 015

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/015-session-04-14.md`; chapter 15; section Memorable Moments

**Buppido reveals the entrance to the Whorlstone Tunnels is hidden within the Derro slums, then launches into a manic rant about the corrupt Council of Savants and his divine mission to free his people.**
- Buppido: "You must let me go. If you don't let me go, the Savants will do something stupid, as they've always done something stupid. I know. I have the divine mission in my hands."

**Droki, a Derro courier with a tentacled floppy hat, is spotted moving with unnatural speed and muttering about being late before vanishing into a hidden fissure behind canvas and moldy boxes.**
- Droki: "Droki is late. They will be so angry. Delays, blocks, procrastinations, obstructions, pfah!"
- Gyrgum: "It's like the rabbit in Alice in Wonderland."
- DM: "He doesn't appear to be on meth." Gyrgum: "Magical, not methical."

**The party discovers the Whorlstone Tunnels, a cavern network thick with faerzress casting swirling spiral patterns of light and muting all sound, filled with size-altering fungi.**
- DM: "The dripping of water makes no echo as if you stood in open air."

**Buppido stands before a makeshift altar of skeletons and bones, demands the party worship him as their divine leader, then flies into a psychotic rage and attacks.**
- Buppido: "I didn't expect you to find me here with my shrine nearly finished and power." … "Yes, I sense you are ready to receive my truth into your hearts."
- Zalthir: "Finds you here? We were with you five seconds ago."
- Buppido: "you will finally give me the worship I deserve."

**Daz unleashes unerring darts of pure magical energy that strike Buppido down, ending his delusions of grandeur and his mad assault on the party.**
- DM: "Buppido's long quest of greatness and his belief in his divine mission has now ended. He's dead."
- Thorin: "He was crazy, but he was our crazy."

**Gyrgum brandishes his chessboard holy symbol, channeling divine power to strike terror into the remaining skeletons and forcing them to flee, allowing the party to mop up the undead.**
- Gyrgum: "The power of Bahamut compels you to get to the point of the matter."
- When the rules required the skeletons to see the symbol, the DM said: "I'm just telling you what the rules are, man. I just adjudicate the rules. I don't write them."

**Hgraam's omens of the evil below.**
- Hgraam: "Something evil stirs in the underdark. The rock itself cries in pain and horror, and the madness creeps from the blackest depths." … "The pebble believes itself flesh. The earth rejects its wards, and the tunnels shake in fury. By these portents, you shall know of evil's presence and face. This is what the stones tell me."

**The party asks for specifics about the evil.**
- Thorin: "Can you be more specific about what kind of evil?" / "How many hit points does it have?"
- Daz: "Is it a swarm of evil?" / "A fog of evil?" / "A fungi of evil."

**Gyrgum sums up Hgraam's point about the Deepking.**
- Gyrgum: "The tighter you squeeze your fist, the more it will slip through your fingers."

**The party commits to following Buppido.**
- Gyrgum: "We'll follow this guy into hell."
- Thorin: "I think we're committed at this point, whether it's a good idea or not."
- Thorin to Buppido: "if you double-cross us, we can still slice you up." Buppido: "but you are now allowing me to proceed onto my grand vision."

**Thorin proposes announcing the party's arrival.**
- Thorin: "We yell out really loud, where Droki at?" … "It's like yelling Maria in West Side Story."

## Chapter 016

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/016-session-04-28.md`; chapter 16; section Memorable Moments

> "The dead have nothing to lose."
> — Thorin

*Thorin conceding, after his intimidation roll failed and the ghost laughed at him, that there is no threatening someone who is already dead.*

> "We can give you a hand."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's deadpan pun after Pelek mentioned that his severed, animated hand had scuttled away through the tunnels wearing an obsidian ring.*

**Pelek's ghost emerges from the ground amid scattered cadavers. He reveals his cleaved head and tells how his trusted companion Buppido lured him in with business deals before slaughtering and dismembering him.**

*A sudden and grim reveal that recontextualized Buppido's madness and gave the party a ghostly new companion.*

> "He seemed like such a charming person. We were going to engage in excellent business deals. He proposed that I come down to his office, and the next thing I know, I'm being slaughtered."
> — Pelek

> "Oh, right. And he's laying right on top of it. Classic Buppido."
> — Gyrgum

*On learning that Buppido's resting place sits right on top of Pelek's.*

**Daz freezes successive sections of the hundred-foot underground pool, creating a makeshift ice bridge that lets the entire party walk safely across the black water.**

*A creative and impressive use of a simple cantrip that solved what seemed like an impassable obstacle. The DM called it a "new creative use of the freeze, the transform water."*

> "Like the Incredibles, the Mr. Freeze guy on him."
> — Daz

> "I tried to possess him just for the record, not possessed."
> — Pelek

*Pelek casually clarifying that Buppido was not possessed by an outside force. Pelek himself had tried to possess Buppido and failed.*

> "Just for the record, watching him go naked was not something I enjoyed."
> — Pelek

> "Get that gnome a robe."
> — Gyrgum

*Pelek corrected him with "Derro, derro, derro," and Gyrgum amended it to "Get that derro a robe, yeah."*

> "I just want you to know, I'm a very well-adjusted ghost."
> — Pelek

> "You Overbrighters call us the Underdark and we call you the Overbright."
> — Pelek

> "The problem with getting to Blingdenstone is not getting there. The problem with getting to Blingdenstone is not getting killed on the way there."
> — Pelek

> "It's like trying to write English without the letter E."
> — Thorin

*Thorin's reaction to Pelek's rule of no eights of anything in Blingdenstone. Gyrgum added: "I might be in trouble. My crossbow does one die eight damage."*

> "That sounds terrible. It does. It's high in calcium, though."
> — Thorin

*Thorin musing, with no real intent, about crushing Pelek's bones into tea as a way to be possessed.*

> "But then again, I did get killed by a crazed derro, so I don't know whether you want to trust my observational skills."
> — Pelek

*After scouting the tunnel beyond the cracks and reporting no traps.*

> "The unassuming pool, the pool of no adjectives."
> — Thorin

> "Ah, this is so much more pleasant than hearing Buppido chew."
> — Pelek

*Pelek, on the whispering sound of the fungi forest.*

**Pelek reveals that the strange magic of the Whorlstone Tunnels animated his severed hand, which scuttled away through the narrow tunnels still wearing his obsidian ring.**

*A creepy and mysterious detail hinting at the dark magical forces pervading the tunnels beneath Gracklstugh.*

**While the party forages peacefully for mushrooms, multiple waves of centipede swarms emerge from the fungi forest and the session ends in chaotic bug-covered combat.**

*The party had just relaxed into a calm foraging session when the ambush struck, with centipedes crawling directly onto Zalthir.*

## Chapter 017

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/017-session-untitled.md`; chapter 17; section Memorable Moments

> "I feel so much better."
> — The Flumph

*Said immediately after flooding the party's minds with a torrent of traumatic visions and unpleasant memories, cheerfully unburdening itself at their expense.*

> "Okay, so he trauma dumped on us."
> — Thorin

> "Well, it's a natural technique of mine. I share my pain and misery, and once I do that, it's no longer mine."
> — The Flumph

*The flumph cheerfully explains its coping mechanism. Later it asks, "What was that expression you used? Trauma dump?" and adopts the term.*

> "Here I am, a creature you've not seen before... And all you could do is just stare at me blankly while I speak into your skulls."
> — The Flumph

*The flumph grows indignant that the stunned party hasn't said a word back to it.*

> "I could float around from the back and offer play-by-play commentary."
> — The Flumph

*Responding to the party's request for combat assistance, offering the least helpful form of support imaginable.*

> "But, you know, it's not all bad. It's a tasty snack. Every time I get a little bit hungry, I just, you know, float by there."
> — The Flumph

*On the mind flayer lurking near the Assassin's Guild hideout.*

**The flumph reveals that Plinki the derro is torturing a stolen red dragon egg with dark rituals, attempting to mutate it into a two-headed mount for the demon lord Demogorgon.**

*A shocking revelation delivered via psychic trauma dump, raising the stakes considerably for the party's mission.*

> "Yeah, I mean, like, to begin with, I don't know if we want them to be mounted... Unless you mean mounted on the wall as trophies."
> — Gyrgum

> "So there's a lot of two-head energy going around right now, is what I'm seeing."
> — Thorin

> "That was monk tradition."
> — the GM, imagining Zalthir

*The table pictured Zalthir frantically stomping on the centipedes crawling all over him; the GM supplied his deadpan excuse, and Zalthir took it up at once: "Long tradition, yes."*

> "Things they teach you in the monastic order, but not how to get somebody to be quiet."
> — the GM

*The GM's aside after Zalthir's monastic studies identified the flumph's psionic-collecting tendril.*

> "Could we like convince them that we're a Demogorgon and they should give us the egg? Do you think that'd work?"
> — Thorin

> "That's the newest form of crypto, proof of egg."
> — Gyrgum

> "I don't think you understand the circumstances of your current employment with me."
> — Gartokkar Xundorn

> "We're just never agreed to any sort of employment, but okay. You know, you do you."
> — Thorin

> "I don't know who taught you how to make deals."
> — Gartokkar Xundorn

*Gyrgum accepted the opening offer of 2,000 gold as a full advance. The short duergar stood up to him and insisted on 30% up front and 70% on delivery. Gyrgum privately noted he had only been going to ask for 20%.*

> "What Themberchaud doesn't know doesn't hurt Themberchaud."
> — Gartokkar Xundorn

*Gartokkar conspiratorially agrees to keep the dragon egg secret from the very dragon his order tends, after a moment in which he looked ready to attack the party.*

> "So yes, our price for that service is double."
> — Thorin

> "You just got to know when you have the advantage. That's all I'm saying."
> — Thorin

> "There will not be a cave, a rock, a tunnel, a pool..."
> — Gartokkar Xundorn

*Gartokkar's menacing threat to the party, enumerating every geological formation in the Underdark where they might try to hide if they fail to deliver.*

**The party realizes the flumph will be joining them whether they want it to or not, having already made itself their new best friend through repeated psychic trauma dumping.**

*After the flumph cheerfully announces it will accompany them, the party resigns itself to having an emotionally needy telepathic companion. Gyrgum: "It's the kind of annoying friend who will never stop talking about stuff that is, you know, uninteresting, even when you have something important to do."*

## Chapter 018

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/018-session-untitled.md`; chapter 18; section Memorable Moments

> "You should have accepted the thousand."
> — Thorin

*After Werz haggled and the party raised the NDA price to 1,100 gold, Thorin delivered this deadpan remark as Werz reluctantly paid the higher sum.*

> "If you question it again, it goes up to 1,100."
> — Daz

*Daz's sharp response after Werz tried to haggle on the NDA price, immediately punishing him for questioning the deal.*

> "You're right. It was insulting. We won't take the thousand."
> — Daz

*Daz turns Werz's own excuse — that accepting a first offer would be insulting — straight back on him to lock in the higher price.*

> "Well, it is, in fact, non-demonic, but in this case, we would accept a small fee not to talk about this agreement with anybody else."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum answering Werz's puzzled "Non-demonic agreement?" while pitching the concept of an NDA to a bewildered Duergar merchant in the middle of the Underdark, complete with ghost-based enforcement.*

> "Is there any curses involved? Any promises of demonic possession? Or is it just one of those I gotta trust you deals?"
> — Werz Saltbaron

*Werz's response to the NDA pitch. Gyrgum stares knowingly at the ghost, and the ghost says "boo."*

**Thorin announces a new character trait of 'radical candor' — then immediately rolls dice to decide whether to blurt out the secret dragon egg to Errde Blackskull, rolling a 20 and keeping his mouth shut.**

*Thorin's player introduces a chaotic new roleplay mechanic where dice determine whether he blurts out sensitive information at the worst possible moments. The GM set the saves on Thorin's Wisdom (+0), and Thorin blurts unless he makes the save: DC 8 for dangerous things, 12 when unsure, 20 for things that seem harmless. "That will encourage you to be mostly blurting things out in random social interactions."*

> "I think just information wants to be free. I think we just tell everybody everything and just let the chips fall where they may."
> — Thorin

*Gyrgum's immediate reply: "That seems like a great way to get killed." Zalthir added: "Just I want to point out the one choosing that now in the Underdark of all places is interesting timing."*

> "I feel like everybody's threatening everybody because they're all looking for a trigger to go on some bloodbath thing. And so the fact that everybody wants to kill us actually means we're probably safe."
> — Daz

*Thorin: "I'm not sure it works like that, but you know." The GM: "That's a very Drow thing to say."*

> "Yeah, but they can be near each other and not working together. I'm standing near you right now."
> — Daz

*Daz undercutting Errde's leap from "they're near each other" to "working together." Errde's reply: "you present a conundrum. Daz. Well played."*

> "Do you know how boring it was to be stuck within 30 feet of your murderer?"
> — The Ghost (Pelek)

*The ghost's answer when Gyrgum suggests it simply haunt the obsidian gem so he can carry it around in his bag.*

> "Well, first of all, assassins are not your enemy. They are professionals. They're paid to do a job."
> — Werz Saltbaron

*The moment that made the party suspect Werz knows far more about the assassins than he admits. Gyrgum: "It sounds like you know, assassins."*

> "So you're just using us as cut-rate assassins."
> — Thorin

*After Thorin asks why Werz doesn't simply hire assassins to kill the assassins, and Werz answers, "That would be expensive."*

> "If you provided the right information to the right people at the right time, it would probably turn into an all-out war."
> — Werz Saltbaron

*Werz gravely warns the party about the powder-keg state of Gracklstugh, underscoring the stakes of every piece of information they share. Thorin, radical-candor advocate: "So telling everybody everything would be bad?"*

> "Could you cause some trauma?"
> — The Flumph

*The flumph, finding that its repeated trauma dumps are "no longer as satisfying," asks the party to generate some fresh trauma — just not on it. Thorin: "I mean, yeah, trauma finds us. Don't worry, dude."*

> "It's in their nature to be violent. They are bred to be violent."
> — Stool

*Stool's view of the quaggoths, which prompts Thorin's "That's eugenics" and Daz's "It is how we ended up with orange carrots."*

> "If the Deepking were unseated, Clan Ironhead would be happy to help those who helped the Deepking's departure."
> — Grinta Ironhead

*Grinta pulls the party aside after their public meeting with her grandfather to make a secret political offer, hinting at the possibility of regime change in Gracklstugh.*

**Werz hands the party five mysterious gems, telling them to ask their gnomish friends why they are special — Jimjar identifies them as empty spell gems vital to the defense of Blingdenstone, raising the question of how Werz obtained them.**

*A seemingly simple gift from a merchant turns into a significant mystery connecting Gracklstugh's underworld to the gnomish city of Blingdenstone.*

## Chapter 019

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/019-session-05-20.md`; chapter 19; section Memorable Moments

**Daz covertly steals a document from Eldgrim's desk bearing the royal seal of Gracklstugh, revealing a secret alliance between the Deepking and the Empty-Scabbard Killers assassins.**

*While Thorin distracted the guards by questioning the logic of the faction's name, Daz slipped away to rifle through papers on the leader's desk.*

> "So you manage to take the piece of paper. And as you're, and it is the important part about it, it is written, it is signed Deepking and bears the royal seal of Gracklstugh."
> — DM

**Daz fakes a violent coughing fit and, on his cue, Zalthir breathes fire onto Eldgrim's desk, incinerating the remaining papers and enraging the assassin leader.**

*To cover Daz's theft, Daz and Zalthir staged the fire together; it got the entire party banished from the assassins' lair.*

> "Like, so I start, I start coughing like I'm like I'm like, like I'm choking on something. My good friend comes over to help me."
> — Daz

> "Maybe I'll become a screenplay writer instead of a book writer."
> — Daz

> "How do you kill people if you have an empty scabbard?"
> — Thorin

*Thorin asks it of Rust and Bob when they first stop the party in the hallway; he returns to the theme in the lair to distract the guards while Daz steals the royal document.*

> "So I was asking Tweedledee and Tweedledum over here. Why do you all call yourselves the empty scabbards? Like, does that mean you don't have any weapons? Or are they invisible?"
> — Thorin

> "If you must use a nickname, you can always use my nickname, Rust."
> — Rust
>
> "Rust? Like the language?"
> — Gyrgum

> "That is a matter of small potatoes. I can permanently whitelist him for a small fee."
> — Eldgrim
>
> "These days, we prefer the term allow list. It's less racist."
> — Gyrgum

> "Ah, yes. We are troubleshooters, you might say."
> — Gyrgum
>
> "Well, we're pretty good at offering non-disclosure services."
> — Gyrgum
>
> "In this case, aren't we looking for the opposite? We want disclosure services."
> — Zalthir
>
> "We deal in information, but also we have swords."
> — Thorin

> "You, I like you. You talk honestly, directly. No wishy-washiness, no ambiguity about where you stand."
> — Eldgrim, to Thorin after Thorin's sarcastic thanks for "free passage"

> "Why am I paying you to take care of your problem?"
> — Daz

> "Do you want me to sign something? I'll sign something. But nobody will believe you."
> — Eldgrim

> "The fame of the Empty-Scabbard Killers will go deep, and the fact that they will enter into your book stories … although the way you're going, you probably won't ever get to write a book. You'll probably be dead."
> — Eldgrim (lightly condensed from the DM's narration)

> "Plausible deniability. That's our group name, plausible deniability."
> — Thorin

**Gyrgum strides boldly into the obelisk chamber and bluffs the fanatical cultist Plinki with a natural 20, convincing her that Demogorgon himself sent a cleric of Bahamut to acknowledge her grand achievement.**

*Gyrgum's audacious gambit bought the party precious time to assess the chamber and position themselves near the corrupted dragon egg.*

> "Ah, I am a cleric of Bahamut come to come to worship at the egg."
> — Gyrgum

> "I knew Demogorgon would be pleased with our thing for he has sent a cleric of Bahamut to acknowledge the greatness of my achievement."
> — Plinki

> "Ah, these are these are these are my companions. They are not so enlightened, but they support me in my efforts, I assume."
> — Gyrgum

> "The blood of a cleric of Bahamut is all that is required to complete the ritual."
> — Plinki

*Plinki's cheerful revelation that she needs Gyrgum's beating heart to finish her demonic ritual instantly shattered the party's diplomatic cover.*

> "You're like, I can spare a drop or two."
> — Thorin
>
> "No, it has to be his beating heart."
> — DM

**The party debates the theology of Bahamut and whether a corrupted, demonic dragon egg could be made into an omelet with two yolks — one dragon, one demon.**

*A moment of absurd levity as the party weighed the moral implications of destroying a demonically possessed dragon egg.*

> "I wonder what a dragon omelette would taste like. Does it have two yolks?"
> — Thorin
>
> "Well, there's the dragon yoke and the demon yoke."
> — Gyrgum

> "Freeze with indecision and get slaughtered."
> — Thorin, supplying the fourth option to Gyrgum's "fight or flee or hide"

**Zalthir grapples Plinki in the darkness and unleashes a flurry of strikes, while Gyrgum blasts her with a powerful necrotic spell, leaving the mad cultist severely wounded as the battle hangs in the balance.**

*The party's coordinated ambush in the magical darkness turned the tide of the opening round of combat against the derro cultist.*

> "I mean, he's egging us on."
> — Gyrgum, closing the session

## Chapter 020

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/020-session-untitled.md`; chapter 20; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir unleashes a 15-foot cone breath attack, killing Plinki outright and blinding several other enemies in a single devastating strike.**

*Zalthir sees through his own magical Darkness and delivers the killing blow to the Derro leader threatening Gyrgum and the egg.*

> "So, GM, am I doing this right? If I can breathe breath in a 15-foot cone, is it like that?"
> — Zalthir's player

> "But it does have a salutary effect. It kills Plinki."
> — GM

> "Hey, well, there's something."
> — Zalthir's player

*The breath's damage roll was low, but it was enough to finish Plinki.*

> "He who has the egg rules!"
> — Derro Cultist

*A crazed Derro shrieks this revelation as the cultists scramble blindly in the darkness, unaware the egg has already been stolen.*

> "Where is the egg? Where is the egg of Demogorgon?"
> — Derro Cultist

> "He who loses the egg drools."
> — Gyrgum's player

> "Kill the Cleric of Bahamut! Sacrifice, cut his heart out! Feed it to Demogorgon!"
> — Derro Cultist

*A fanatical Derro screams this battle cry at Gyrgum, with the ghost immediately responding in a terrifying voice.*

> "I'm all like, no, do not." / "Yes, we will." / "Ooh. That was the ghost. It's scary."
> — Gyrgum's player and GM (exchange)

> "Everyone always wants to kill the cleric."
> — Gyrgum's player, when the cry went up again

**Zalthir strikes a Derro and pushes him off a ten-foot ledge, killing him instantly in a move compared to the iconic scene from the film 300.**

*Zalthir's strike pushes a Derro off the ten-foot ledge to his death, and the table calls it the scene from 300.*

> "I'm imagining it's like that scene from that movie 300 where they like kick the dude off"
> — Gyrgum's player

> "it would be the quiet version of the 300"
> — speaker uncertain (merged cue)

**Daz secretly snatches the egg and hides it under his robes, then Misty Steps away with a whoosh while the Derro cultists frantically fumble in the darkness looking for it.**

*The Derro scream about the missing egg while Daz has already teleported away with it concealed on his person, leaving them completely oblivious.*

> "Yes, and he misty-stepped, so it's not like he ran away. They hear a whoo."
> — GM

**The party's ghost ally flies across the battlefield and possesses the body of the enemy spy, causing his eyes to glow as he falls under spectral control.**

*Daz cries out for help as the spy corners him, and the ghost responds by abandoning its own form to inhabit the spy's body, effectively neutralizing the threat.*

> "And periodically, you can see his eyes glow like a Goa'uld from Stargate."
> — GM

> "The ghost is now in possession of the spy. Right. We have a new party member now."
> — GM

**Thorin uses his Sentinel ability to land an opportunity attack on the Master Thief as he tries to flee, reducing his speed to zero and trapping him in place for the killing blow.**

*The Master Thief attempts to disengage and escape after a brutal series of grapples and attacks, only to be stopped cold by Thorin's sentinel stance.*

> "I thought with Sentinel, if you hit them with an opportunity attack, then they can't move."
> — Zalthir's player, catching the rule Thorin's player had forgotten

> "Okay, well, he sits there. Good job, Zalthir."
> — GM

> "I mean, this is definitely a Jackie Chan movie."
> — GM, as Zalthir finally drops the pinned Master Thief

**The GM praises the party's lockdown of the Master Thief.**

> "Yes, but like Zalthir and the darkness and the ghost have managed to selectively neutralize him in a truly epic way. In a way that I admire and enjoy. I think it's like a very clever use of the terrain."
> — GM

**The party's companions can't hit anything.**

> "At this point in time, the other creatures are saying these guys can't hit the stormtroopers."
> — GM

> "It's best if you're a spy not to run around with a name tag."
> — Gyrgum's player, as the spy flees

## Chapter 021

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/021-session-untitled.md`; chapter 21; section Memorable Moments

> "Our heroes have once again saved the day, or at the very least, created new problems for themselves."
> — DM

*The session's opening line.*

> "I think I'd like an option where nobody goes nuts and tries to kill everybody."
> — Gyrgum

*The DM had just listed which faction would "go nuts and try to kill everybody" depending on what the party told them.*

> "Go not to the elves for advice. They will say both yes and no."
> — Gyrgum

*The DM had warned that Gyrgum's superiors in the sect of Bahamut would give him "12 contradictory opinions" about demonic dragons.*

> "There's plenty of time for us to pop potato it to someone else."
> — Gyrgum

*After learning the egg won't hatch for 44 weeks.*

> "We do kind of want a dragon party member with us this time."
> — Thorin

*Wishing for a baby dragon of their own, with Zalthir the dragonborn standing right there. The DM needled that Zalthir was "feeling a little bit like, you know, dissed right now," and Gyrgum called it "a very blood purity kind of a thing." Thorin: "I didn't realize it came out that way. I'm sorry."*

> "It fell off the back of the truck and we gave it back."
> — Gyrgum

*His proposed explanation to the Keepers of the Flame for how the egg came home.*

> "Yeah, taking the list and checking it twice and keeping it to ourselves."
> — Gyrgum
