> "No, we're going to align with ourselves."
> — Thorin

*On campaigns that seem to force you to pick a faction. The DM replied that "there's always another option."*

**The party discovers a heavy baby rattle inscribed with the name of Clan Thrazgad, clearly crafted for a dragon to chew on, raising unsettling questions about who commissioned it and why.**

*Found among sparse loot in the Council lab, the rattle's existence implies a Duergar smithing clan had dealings with whoever was raising the dragon.*

> "Is there a baby? No, there's no baby, but there is a baby rattle."
> — DM

> "Sometimes a rattle is just a rattle."
> — Thorin

> "You do not know who we are."
> — Daz

*Stepping up to the terrified Derro guards before the party had a name. Daz was surrounded by spinning orbs of light and trembling ground; the name was voted on by committee moments later. He described the lights as "the, you know, shock and awe part of us."*

**Daz's magical light display and Gyrgum's tremors convince the Derro guards that Daz is a 20th-level drow archmage, and the legend of the Ember Vanguard is born.**

*The party improvised an entire reputation on the spot, leaving the guards alive to spread a wildly exaggerated tale of their power. Daz's natural 20 carried a failed roll from Thorin, and the Derro mistook the floating embers for a meteor swarm.*

> "I'm happy to be underestimated."
> — Thorin

> "I hope I don't actually run into somebody that's a 20-level wizard because I'll get my ass kicked."
> — Daz

> "Oh, he took the day off."
> — Gyrgum

*His answer to anyone who asks where the 20th-level mage went.*

**The party spends several minutes debating group names live at the table, consulting ChatGPT and Notebook LM, before settling on 'Ember Vanguard' with the endorsement 'I don't have anything better.'**

*A spontaneous branding session erupted mid-session as the party realised they needed a name to intimidate the Derro guards. Rejected candidates included "The Dazzling Delvers," "Over and Up," "The Abyssal Scourge," "The Rift Wardens," "Light Bound" (which Thorin said "sounds like a VC firm"), "Hell No," "The Madness Breakers," and "The Valiant Vanguard."*

> "This is why nobody ever lets me in name products."
> — Gyrgum

*After echoing Thorin's "Over and up."*

> "Consider it a banger social media post."
> — Thorin

**The Trapper strikes the rear of the party and envelops Gyrgum completely, smothering him in acid and crushing weight, reducing him to a single hit point through sheer orcish will.**

*An ambush from a camouflaged predator catches the party off guard, with Gyrgum only surviving due to his racial ability to refuse death.*

> "I've never been enveloped. What is it like?"
> — Gyrgum

> "The mechanic is grapple. The vibe is envelop."
> — Gyrgum

*Summing up the DM's ruling, "The crunch is grapple. The aesthetic is big creature enveloping you."*

> "All right, guys, I'm in trouble."
> — Gyrgum

*After failing his escape roll with a 9.*

**Zalthir counter-grapples the massive Trapper with his bare hands, pinning the creature that had swallowed his ally, and beats it to death with a flurry of unarmed strikes.**

*Zalthir used his grappler expertise to turn the creature's own tactic against it, landing the killing blow and freeing a battered, acid-covered Gyrgum. Gyrgum called the grapple-on-grapple "a big three-way hug."*

> "I can't abide that sort of icky stuff, you know?"
> — Gyrgum

*The acid-covered orc, scraping himself clean while everyone else was perplexed to see him find something disgusting.*

### Memorable Moments

> "Look, if you wanted to the whole list of who we work for, I've got that in a database and we can go over it together."

*His deadpan reply when the derro savant Ulnara demanded to know who the party worked for. It baffled the insane cultist.*

>
> "It says inner join."
> — Thorin
>
> "I'm not good at the database stuff, to be honest."

*The database bit escalated. Daz had already told Ulnara she would need to pay them before they would spend time on it.*

> "No, I'm just here to check up on the zombies."
> "How's the big project coming along?"
> — Gyrgum

*The opening of the supervisor ruse on Skiit.*

> "Only if you succeed at this one. I'm still seeing a lot of zombies that haven't finished their tasks down there. You got to keep them on plan."
> — Gyrgum

*His reply after Skiit complained that Plinki had promised him a better job.*

**Gyrgum's supervisor bluff completely fooled the derro Skiit, right up until he rolled a natural one on his intimidation or persuasion check. The cultist puffed up and accused the party of being spies.**

*The ruse was working perfectly until the dice had other plans, and a smooth deception turned into a chaotic confrontation.*

> "I'm a follower of Demogorgon and Plinki and what's Plinki has said that anybody who follows Demogorgon is going to make the derro proud and we're going to be great again."
> — Skiit

*His puffed-up outburst after the natural one, followed by: "You work for the duergar or maybe the mind flayers."*

> "I got the low end of the stick. I always get the low end of the stick."
> — Skiit

*Muttered while tossing body parts to the zombies, just before he turned and asked, "Are you more body parts for the pit?"*

> "I feel like my supervisor ploy worked pretty well right up until the point I rolled a natural one."
> — Gyrgum

> "Do not worry. Do not worry. After he's dead, I will turn him into a zombie so that he can serve the cult of Demogorgon indefinitely."
> — Ulnara

*Her chilling, matter-of-fact response after watching the party crush her subordinate Skiit with an earthen hand.*

> "I see that Skiit was a bit was a great disappointment to you, your lordship. Was it the Ember Vanguard? What a wonderful name for such a powerful group."
> — Ulnara

*A bureaucrat's sudden deference once Daz's violence landed.*

> "The Ember again Vanguard has given me excellent supplies. I am sure you will communicate to Plinki that Ulnara was nothing but a wonderful ally in the machinations of the great cult."
> — Ulnara
>
> "We shall certainly convey all appropriate thoughts."
> — Gyrgum

**Daz conjured a hand of compacted earth to crush the fallen Skiit at the bottom of the pit, then finished him off with a burst of necrotic energy, all to impress a borderline-insane cult leader.**

*A calculated display of violence that earned the trust of the derro savant Ulnara and avoided a fight with her zombie horde.*

> "A scale, smooth as polished obsidian, but it shimmered like madness in the dim light."
> — Derro cultist at the cauldron

*Part of the rehearsed speech he recited, broke off, and started again: "That's not right. Not right."*

> "I say that I worship Bahamut."
> — Gyrgum

*His answer when a derro patrol asked if the party worshipped the dual-headed master. It immediately started a full combat encounter. His follow-up: "I was thinking maybe we'd have a philosophical discussion or something."*

> "You know, these gods are not unrelated. It's just that one of them has two heads, the other has one. Really, you should be prepared to discuss this kind of thing as I parry whatever it is."
> — Gyrgum

*Spoken mid-combat while a derro screamed about death to Bahamut. He parried with his armor class of 14.*

> "With one head is better than two."
> — Gyrgum

*His arrogant parting remark after killing the last derro, who had attacked over a theological dispute about Bahamut versus Demogorgon.*

**The party discovers books made from trillimac mushroom caps filled with mad scribblings on a glowing ritual platform. They may be the physical evidence of demonic conspiracy that Errde Blackskull has been seeking.**

*The party spots this possible key evidence while sneaking past a death dog and derro cultists in the brimstone cavern.*

> "Stand back. I am reading the book. None of you can read the book."
> — Narrak (paraphrase of GM narration: "He goes, stand back. I am reading the book. None of you can read the book.")

## Chapter 023

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/023-session-untitled.md`; chapter 23; section Memorable Moments

> "Silence, you blithering half-wits! More vigor! This city's doom, a ritual! Dig it!"
> — Narrak

*Narrak, the squeaky-voiced Derro cult leader, furiously berating his followers for their lack of passion during the ritual chant rehearsal*

> "Just because I involved demonic powers and tried to cause mass casualty events on inside of Gracklstugh does not mean I have to curse."
> "Gosh darn it."
> — Narrak

*The DM roleplaying Narrak as a prim, profanity-averse cultist who draws the line at swearing despite summoning demons. Earlier he explained: "I don't curse. I just invoke demonic powers."*

> "He actually says, by golly."
> — Zalthir's player
>
> "Yes, I say, by golly."
> — Narrak
>
> "I love this guy."
> — Zalthir's player

*Zalthir's player falling for the villain.*

> "Hear me, you two-headed fools! … From Whorlstone's depths, the spirals turn. For Demogorgon, the city will burn. More heads for grafting, more for the pile."
> — Narrak
>
> "Two heads, two heads, a glorious sight. The Deepking falls into endless night. Narrak's vision, sharp and keen, the prince of chaos, always unseen."
> — Derro Cultists (chorus)

*The ritual chant in full. Gyrgum asked who exactly "two-headed fools" was addressed to, since none of those present had two heads. The DM: "I have no idea."*

> "Wow. Can we just like sit here and watch? I want to see what's going to happen next. This is great."
> — Zalthir

*The party, enjoying the rehearsal, briefly considers just letting the show continue*

> "I haven't slept like that in ages. Strictly speaking, I haven't slept since I was dead. That's part of the problem."
> — Pelek

*Pelek waking up refreshed after his first night of sleep in a physical body, cheerfully noting the irony of his ghostly existence*

> "I just want to point out that we've completed a full circle. Will we ever leave these ghastly caverns?"
> — Pelek

*Answered by Daz's confident "Look, we got out of the underdark before," which drew Zalthir's "Wait, we did?"*

> "I suggest you tie me up before I go to sleep. There is the small infinitesimal possibility that the entity that I'm currently possessing might recapture my body."
> — Pelek

> "This could be a new policy. Always tie up new members."
> — Zalthir
>
> "Yeah, we always tie up the new members."
> — Daz

> "Bahamut has told you, take the damn statue to Hgraam. But you know what? He also believes in free will."
> — DM

*After Gyrgum's plan to fix the second head with a chisel ("Well, what if I used a magic chisel?" … "Go to Magical Home Depot.")*

> "You're beginning to realize that if Narrak didn't have a squeaky voice, it would sound a lot more ominous."
> — DM

**Daz realizes that tying up Pelek is unnecessary, since the ghost's possession is stable unless the host is knocked unconscious, but deliberately withholds this information to keep the untrustworthy newcomer restrained.**

*Recalling knowledge of undead possession mechanics, Daz quietly decides the party's safety outweighs full transparency with their new ghostly companion: "the devil we know versus the one we don't."*

**Narrak unleashes a wave of magical fear that sends Thorin, Gyrgum, and Zalthir fleeing in terror, nearly unraveling the party's ambush.**

*Just as the party's surprise attack seemed to be going well, Narrak turned the tide by terrorizing three of the four party members simultaneously. Gyrgum had blessed everyone except himself.*

**Daz conjures a massive hand of compacted earth that erupts from the ground to seize and restrain Narrak, breaking his concentration and ending his Fear.**

*After the party struggled against Narrak's fear spell, Daz pinned the cult leader in place with earthen magic, which broke Narrak's concentration and ended the Fear.*

**The party spends several minutes debating how heavy a stone giant statue would be before realizing it is only two feet tall and easily carriable.**

*Everyone assumed the statues were life-sized giants until the DM clarified they were miniatures, instantly resolving the logistical crisis. Gyrgum: "That's the lightest giant I've ever heard of." The DM then shrank the map tokens, and they promptly turned invisible on everyone's screen.*

**The death dog's handler hides from his furious boss by grooming the dog.**

*The DM explains the cultist's stealth-blind distraction as office politics: "He goes, no, no, I'm taking care of the death dog… Office politics. It doesn't change."*

## Chapter 024

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/024-session-untitled.md`; chapter 24; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir activates his Eldritch Claw Tattoo (Eldritch Maul), and his face and arms transform into writhing inky tentacles that reach 15 feet into the magical darkness to grapple and pummel the ettin. No one outside the sphere can see it.**

*Zalthir's player reveals his carefully planned character build, which combines darkness, tentacles, and Tavern Brawler grappling into a devastating combo.*

> "So while I was frightened, I activated my Eldritch Maul, which means my face turn into tentacles."
> — Zalthir

> "Sadly, nobody sees it because it's in the darkness."
> — Zalthir

> "Where the fuck did you get an Eldritch?"
> — Thorin

> "It's a special power of my tattoo. It glows a little bit."
> — Zalthir

> "Each of your melee attacks with an unarmed strike can reach a target up to 15 feet away from you as inky tendrils launch toward the target."
> — Zalthir, reading the tattoo's description

> "I mean, just so we're clear, there's these black things that kind of go from your arms and your kind of hands become disembodied as these black things hold on. Kind of creepy."
> — GM

> "I believe you have officially come up with the only useful use of the tavern brawler feet."
> — GM (transcribed "feet" for "feat")

*The DM reacts with impressed surprise as Zalthir's master plan of grappling enemies into magical darkness with tentacles comes together.*

> "He can actually make the circle of darkness, walk towards the edge a little bit, pull people into the darkness, grapple them, and beat their ass. I like this."
> — Daz

**Narrak, restrained and dying, gasps his final words: the ritual cannot be undone, and someone has been sacrificed. The party is left with an ominous and unresolved mystery.**

*The party expected a villain's death speech. Instead they got a cryptic dying declaration hinting at consequences already set in motion.*

> "No, the ritual cannot be undone. … sacrificed."
> — Narrak

*Narrak's dying words, spoken with fading conviction, hint that the party's victory may have come too late to stop the ritual's consequences.*

> "Fools, your sacrifice is but a step toward glory."
> — Narrak, as his cultists begin to fall

> "I will not be held in prison indefinitely. I will escape."
> — Narrak, straining against the earthen hand (and failing)

> "Seriously, Narrak doesn't say anything." / "I was waiting for a whole speech. Come on."
> — GM and Gyrgum, when a cultist dies silently mid-fight

**Stool pipes up immediately after the battle to remind Zalthir, with the patience of a seven-year-old, that they still need to go rescue Rumpadump.**

*The party has barely finished looting the bodies when the small fungal creature reasserts its singular priority with cheerful persistence.*

> "So are we going to go save my friend Rumpadump now, Zalthir?"
> — Stool

**The party realizes they have now killed two members of the Council of Savants and jokes about claiming two seats on the council. They are then reminded that they would need to actually be derro.**

*Post-battle banter as the party tallies up the political fallout of their actions in the Underdark.*

> "Is this the type of thing where if I kill him, I get to become on the council, right?"
> — Thorin

> "Oh, so I get two seats."
> — Thorin

> "This isn't like a venture capital firm where just because you threw out the last two guys, you get the two board seats. Like, you have to be a derro."
> — GM

> "I figure that if we kill enough of them, we get to make our own rules about membership, though."
> — Gyrgum

**Gyrgum is knocked prone and his concentration breaks, and his carefully maintained acid spell fizzles. He then shrugs off the follow-up attacks from the surrounding cultists with his armor.**

*A single hooked spear strike cascades into lost concentration and a fall to the ground, briefly turning the tide against the party's cleric.*

> "I seem to be prone to failing."
> — Gyrgum

> "At this point, Gyrgum must be thinking, I am, Bahamut protects me from these cultists of evil."
> — GM, after a crossbow bolt misses Gyrgum

> "That's right. I am invulnerable."
> — Gyrgum

> "Finally, my spell did something useful."
> — Gyrgum, when Tasha's Caustic Brew finishes off the acid-coated derro

**The cultists abandon the ritual for violence, and the party treats the fight like a bad meeting.**

> "They didn't want to hang out for the audit?"
> — Thorin

> "No, we didn't offer an audit this time."
> — Daz

> "Could have been an email."
> — Thorin, as the GM notes a figure in the back corner "really trying hard to ignore the meeting"

## Chapter 025

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/025-session-untitled.md`; chapter 25; section Memorable Moments

> "you're absolutely right. You're completely right. Software has huge cost overruns, delays, catastrophic releases, and then there are layoffs and cutbacks."
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir's player's out-of-game advice on how to handle a contractor who tried to compare construction delays to software development, turning the argument back on him*

*The DM tempering the party's excitement over their pile of blackmail material*

*Zalthir pitching the dead man's switch. When Thorin's player suggested just telling people they had one, Zalthir replied, "I know, but it would be even better if it were true."*

> "Rumpadump would never let that happen!"
> — Stool

*Stool passionately defending his friend Rumpadump as the party debated whether to bother going to check on the infected Myconids*

> "Thorin, Thorin, you're such a — you're much nicer than Zalthir. I'm going to be your friend now."
> — Stool

*Stool turning to Thorin for support after Zalthir refuses to go, much to Zalthir's chagrin. Zalthir's reply: "Okay, this is called splitting. I'm not here for it."*

> "Zalthir is just scared. Zalthir's a scaredy cat. He thinks all my conds are bad."
> — Stool

> "Can we just tell him it's bedtime again? Like, it worked last time."
> — Daz

*The DM's answer: "Nice try."*

> "Does anybody ever kill somebody so that they have a new helper?"
> — Daz

*Asked after Stool explained that Myconid "zombies" are really a word meaning "creature that helps after death." Stool's flat "No." drew: "You don't sound confident."*

> "Unless they have two heads."
> — Zalthir

*Responding to Daz's recollection that Myconids are generally quite peaceable*

> "I'm not a mushroom. I'm a Myconid."
> — Rumpadump

*Rumpadump indignantly setting the record straight after Daz asked whether myconids eating mushrooms was cannibalism. Daz recovered with: "Don't you know the difference between an ape and a monkey?"*

> "So we have the mushrooms that drive myconids crazy. According to a six-year-old equivalent myconid."
> — Zalthir

> "oh man hey beautiful soul you've been grooving through the dark for a while now … dig this there's a far out place a hidden trip where the lady herself spins the vibes it's like the ultimate underground rave but in your mind man"
> — Voosbur

> "You ready to drop in, tune out, and dance with the spores? … Peace, love, and mushroom, the man. Will you groove with the lady's dream?"
> — Voosbur

*The DM's improvised hippie monologue. Asked whether it was module text, he said: "If that was text from the module, I'd be seriously impressed … since we've hyped it up so much, I felt like it had to have more depth."*

**Thorin impulsively takes Voosbur's outstretched hand and enters Zuggtmoy's hallucinatory spore dreamscape, with Gyrgum following to keep watch.**

*After rolling a die to decide his character's fate, Thorin's player committed to the reckless choice, deeply uncomfortable but fully in character*

> "It's deeply uncomfortable for me, GM. I hope you give me points for that."
> — Thorin

> "somebody needs to be the designated driver here."
> — Gyrgum

> "Well, maybe we shouldn't have split the party."
> — Gyrgum

*As the vines of the Vine Wood began reaching for them*

> "I'd say hello, lady, as Andre the Giant from the Princess Bride."
> — Thorin

> "Welcome, wanderers of flesh and bone. You tread the boundary between waking and dreaming, where life and decay entwine in endless dance."
> — Zuggtmoy

> "Do you fear the rot, or do you understand its song?"
> — Zuggtmoy

> "Maybe next time, lady, but I think I got to pass right now. I have too many responsibilities back in the other world."
> — Thorin

*Thorin refusing Zuggtmoy's offer of eternal corruption after feeling genuinely tempted, standing on the edge of a life-altering demonic deal*

> "You could have lingered with me and found your heart entwined with mine, forever part of the endless mycelium."
> — Zuggtmoy

> "Hey, man, I guess you didn't want to join the party. I got to go now."
> — Voosbur

**Sarith becomes suddenly agitated upon seeing the dancing Myconids and begins speaking of a 'Dark Lady' calling to him, revealing he has been infected by Zuggtmoy's influence.**

*The party realizes their drow companion's long-standing instability has a demonic cause, raising questions about how long he has been under her influence*

> "The most important level, the Dungeons and Dragons for Magic Users, is the fifth level. Because you finally get Fireball."
> — DM

*Followed by the DM's story of a 30th-level first-edition magic user who killed himself casting a full-power fireball in a 10-by-10 room full of illusory dragons. "There was much discussion about fairness and fun and so on. And so we decided that that room didn't happen."*

> "If RPGBOT says anything other than Fireball, I do not believe them anymore."
> — Daz

**The party emerges from days in the caverns only to be immediately stopped at the sealed city gates by a Duergar guard demanding to search their bag full of demonic artifacts and political dirt.**

*The party's response was to claim diplomatic immunity for their 'diplomatic pouch,' ending the session on a tense and comedic cliffhanger*

> "We're like, sorry, this is a diplomatic pouch. You can't inspect it."
> — Thorin

> "I mean, we can share some mushrooms with him."
> — Daz

## Chapter 026

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/026-session-untitled.md`; chapter 26; section Memorable Moments

**Thorin takes Jimjar's wager and promptly, "accidentally," lets his belt unbuckle and his pants fall in the middle of Laduguer's Furrow, much to the deep gnome's amusement.**
- Thorin: "Yeah, and then I accidentally, my belt unbuckles and my pants fall down."
- Jimjar: "Yeah, that was worth the gold piece."

**Zalthir spots five invisible Duergar soldiers hidden in the hallway at the West Cleft checkpoint, alerting the party to the hidden threat before negotiations turned violent.**
- DM: "That's why the guard is standing very proud and tough, like acting as if he's standing alone in front of you."

**Zalthir swiftly eliminates the visible Derro assassin leader and another hidden foe in rapid succession as the ambush begins in the dark city passageway.**

**Gyrgum summons a swarm of spectral angelic guardians that tear through two Derro assassins during the ambush, turning the tide of the skirmish.**
- Gyrgum: "I cast my brand new spell. Got to try it out called Spirit Guardians."
- Gyrgum: "This is quite useful."

**The party discovers a letter from the Council of Savants on the fallen assassins, revealing the Derro's true target and the political conspiracy lurking beneath Gracklstugh's streets.**
- DM: "Your announcement of who you are had consequences."

**The guard at the gate identifies every species in the party, then reasons his way to the wrong conclusion.**
- Guard: "And you are not a red dragonborn."
- Guard: "You must not be the Ember Vanguard."
- Gyrgum: "Boy, he's good at identifying species."

**Thorin plays dumb about the party's own rumored alter ego.**
- Thorin: "Oh, who's the Ember Vanguard? Tell us about these fearsome people that you're describing. We don't even know who they are."

**Gorglak's ultimatum at the gate.**
- Gorglak: "Because passes mean little here."
- Gorglak: "I've been warned to expect nothing less than fire and shadow. Show me proof you are not the Ember Vanguard or confess and surrender."
- Thorin: "How do we prove a negative?"

**Gyrgum prepares for the salute of his lifetime.**
- Gyrgum: "Stretches a little bit, does some vocal exercises, and gets right into it."
- When Gorglak concedes that they are not the Ember Vanguard, Gyrgum replies: "Oh, fuck those guys."

**Gyrgum on Gorglak's bribe.**
- Gyrgum: "It sounds to me like he wants the kind of gold that's green and folds."

**Daz's egg-hiding plan.**
- Daz: "I'm telling you, dragon omelette."

**Gartokkar loses patience with Daz's coyness.**
- Gartokkar: "Are you a fool?"
- Gartokkar: "Is anyone else here going to say something coherent before this fool irritates me anymore?"
- Gyrgum: "Yes, we have obtained the egg and it is glorious."

**Thorin's no-warranty sales pitch.**
- Thorin: "Here's the deal: we'll give you the egg as we found it, but we make no promises as to the viability of the egg."

**Gartokkar's version of the Ember Vanguard legend, and Thorin's reply.**
- Gartokkar: "A mercenary band so fierce the stone guards fear to face them head-on. Four of them, they say, but not mere adventurers, more like shadowy assassins from Menzoberranzan with magic and blades that rend souls. How did you manage to survive them?"
- Thorin: "We just appear small and insignificant."
- Gartokkar: "Wise. Very wise."

## Chapter 027

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/027-session-untitled.md`; chapter 27; section Memorable Moments

**The party hitches a ride on the back of Themberchaud, the red dragon Wyrmsmith of Gracklstugh, soaring over the city and being mistaken for the legendary Ember Vanguard by the terrified populace below.**

*A desperate plan to bypass the city gates turned into an unexpected dragon ride that sent the entire city into a panic.*

> "This sounds dangerous and stupid, but I mean, on the other hand, dragon ride, so I'll do it."
> — Gyrgum

> "I'm like, wow, I'm going to eat out on this for a month."
> — Gyrgum

**Several party members lose their grip and begin falling from Themberchaud's back mid-flight as the portly dragon attempts an inelegant banking turn.**

*The dragon's lack of grace and excess weight made for a chaotic and undignified ride for the adventurers clinging to his back.*

> "And he's like trying to make it in this elegant way, but with his belly, it kind of turns into this cling."
> — DM

> "If I let go while he's holding it, do I lose the bet?"
> — Jimjar, holding the rope Gyrgum was dangling from

> "My agents. Hail. Themberchaud, father of the flame, has observed your unhurried progress. Your tasks for me are surely taking too long with such inefficient methods."
> — Themberchaud

> "Nonsense. Of course. You are my agents, bound by my protection. I shall provide my express service to where you must go next. Climb aboard, unless you wish to question the will of your Wyrmsmith."
> — Themberchaud

*The vain, out-of-shape dragon grandly offering the party a ride as though it were a great honor, completely unaware he was being manipulated.*

> "My agents have more pressing matters than to loiter among the common rabble and petty traders of the Darklake."
> — Themberchaud

> "But if you look at us, we're about as little as you get. And we'll find the devil in the details."
> — Gyrgum, flattering Themberchaud into dropping them at the docks

> "I, Themberchaud, the Wyrmsmith, bringer of the flame."
> — Themberchaud, before lighting the Forge of Thrazgad

**Gyrgum hands a Duergar guard a caltrop from his pack, solemnly declaring it the secret symbol of their conspiracy and 'deputizing' him on the spot.**

*A brilliantly absurd improvised bluff that convinced a terrified guard he had been recruited into a secret mission, buying the party time to escape.*

> "You are one drow, one dragonborn. You are the Ember Vanguard. Themberchaud has allied himself with the Ember Vanguard. I must warn Captain Errde."
> — Duergar Guard

> "We're impersonating the Ember Vanguard. See, that's really, see, we're not really them, but it's important for our mission that we look a little bit like them."
> — Gyrgum

> "I advise him to file down the points a little bit. They're kind of sharp."
> — Gyrgum

*Deadpan advice offered immediately after he handed a caltrop to a guard as a fake badge of office.*

> "Brilliant. It's a clever scheme."
> — Duergar Guard

> "We better be as powerful as they think we are if we come back."
> — Thorin

> "I can't even remember all the people we've promised and betrayed."
> — Gyrgum

**Jimjar bets the party that the Neverlight Grove's 'circle of joy' will reveal itself to be a 'circle of unwilling vows' before they leave it, hinting at a dark fate awaiting them there.**

*The gnome's ominous wager cast a shadow over the party's planned visit to the Myconid home, suggesting something sinister lurks within.*

> "I bet you an empty shell that this circle of joy will reveal itself to be a circle of unwilling vows before you leave it."
> — Jimjar

> "Are we going to be forced to get married?"
> — Zalthir

> "No, I mean, this is clearly going to go pear-shaped."
> — Thorin, declining the bet

> "How about we go to the tomb first, get some super powerful magical item that may be able to slay demons, and then we check out the demonic infested stool area."
> — Zalthir

**The party intentionally sinks their own boat near the hidden beach, planning to use a mending cantrip to repair it later, which prompts a lengthy discussion about why no wizard ever bothers to learn the mending cantrip.**

*A clever escape plan spiraled into a comedic tangent about naval warfare, wizard recruitment, and the social consequences of knowing utility magic.*

> "That's easy. We'll sink the boat. Then you'll use the mending to fix it."
> — Sarith

> "It's a wonder that a boat ever sinks in this world."
> — Gyrgum

> "But if you suddenly realize that, hey, I know the mending cantrip, therefore I'm prime cannon fodder for the Navy."
> — DM

## Chapter 028

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/028-session-untitled.md`; chapter 28; section Memorable Moments

> "We're not going back."
> — Fuurm

*Fuurm holds up a glowing crystal as a makeshift weapon upon seeing Daz, a drow, approach him in the dark of the Underdark.*

> "We're not trying to take you back anymore."
> — Daz

*Daz's first answer to the terrified escapees. He follows it with: "We found ourselves in a similar situation to the situation I think you just came from, and we're not going back either."*

> "I don't like slaves. I don't like the idea of slavery."
> — Daz

*Daz's reaction on recognising the Drow slave markings on Fuurm and Baedora. The GM muses that this may be part of why he left Menzoberranzan.*

> "So, what I'm hearing is that you should throw it at least 10 feet."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's dry takeaway from Sarith's explanation of the blinding flash crystals. Sarith simply answers, "yes."*

**Zalthir suggests smashing the remaining crystals under magical darkness to disguise the harvest, turning a tactical problem into a clever solution that fools the Drow pursuers.**

*Faced with Eldeth's warning that harvesting crystals would leave an obvious trail, Zalthir devises a plan to make it look like a battle occurred instead.*

> "I mean, can't we, like, I don't know, set off a fireball or earthquake or something to make it not obvious that we cut the crystals and more just there's damage all over. There was a battle."
> — Zalthir

> "Strategically smash in darkness."
> — Zalthir

> "I've always loved smashing things that I can't see."
> — Gyrgum

> "I thought I was going to have to survive throwing a crystal at the shriek."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's confusion when the GM calls for a Survival check right after he throws a crystal at the shriekers.*

**Just before the party arrives at the tomb, Jimjar feels compelled to make a bet, and Gyrgum names the terms: the twelve harvested crystals will come in handy after all. A startled Jimjar takes it.**

*Jimjar's gambling urge meets a player who names the wager first; he takes it, then can find no one to take his follow-up bet.*

> "I bet that these crystals will come in handy after all."
> — Gyrgum

> "I will make a bet that we won't agree on whether they were useful or not."
> — Jimjar

> "Hello? Is someone there? Oh, please, I need your help. I have been trapped in the dark for so long. So very long. Please, won't you help me? Won't you help to free me?"
> — The Voice

*The soft feminine voice first reaches the party's minds as they near the Lost Tomb of Khaem.*

> "I throw a crystal and then I collect on my bet."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum tries to cash in on the Jimjar wager in the dark outside the tomb. The GM: "No, no, this is not the time."*

> "Whereas I'm a big orc."
> — Gyrgum

*Settling the marching order down the long stair, after the GM points out that Daz, as a magic user, is "more fungible in a disaster."*

> "Real adventure is all of our companions who died along the way."
> — Gyrgum

*On the thought that Fargas Rumblefoot, the last survivor of his adventuring group, may not be the most reliable witness to why no one else ever came back.*

> "I bet you can sell a lot of cheeseburgers in a tomb."
> — Gyrgum

*Response to the GM's "you go into a lost tomb and what are you going to get? You know, cheeseburgers."*

**Gyrgum presents his holy symbol boldly to turn the specters, only for every remaining specter to resist, prompting him to wonder if Bahamut is still displeased about the dragon egg.**

*After a dramatic buildup, Gyrgum's divine power fails completely, and the GM pointedly reminds him of a past moral failing involving a dragon egg.*

> "I'm very disappointed in the holy symbol. I shake it sadly."
> — Gyrgum

> "I shake my head in disappointment, but no one can see it, not even me."
> — Gyrgum

*Delivered from inside Zalthir's magical darkness, right after the GM's reminder: "Somewhere you feel that Bahamut was not pleased with how you felt, how you treated the little dragon egg."*

> "Don't worry, Daz, I've got you covered."
> — Thorin

*Thorin kills one specter, then moves to the one draining Daz and hits it for 11. It survives, and he adds: "well, not that well covered. You may want to think about getting out of here."*

> "Specter, I am a specter."
> — A Specter

*The undead corrects Zalthir after he announces he will "punch the ghost." Gyrgum: "These guys are not only undead, they're pretentious."*

> "Your deaths will free me from this prison, and how my victims will serve me even in death. Ha ha ha!"
> — The Wraith

*The wraith manifests in the lower chamber and delivers a cackling villain's monologue the moment the party enters her domain.*

> "I shake my head happily this time."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum confirms the wraith is also undead, and apparently holds out new hope for his holy symbol.*

**Daz prepares to hurl a fireball at the wraith, only to recall mid-cast that she is resistant to fire, and all of his spells are fire-based.**

*Daz enthusiastically announces a fireball, then realizes the wraith resists fire and that his entire spell repertoire is fire-themed.*

> "I was contemplating firing a fireball at his ass."
> — Daz

> "I have all fire stuff."
> — Daz

> "You better watch out. I know where he lives."
> — Gyrgum

*When the wraith slips into its gilded sarcophagus. Once the GM explains it is hiding, Gyrgum adds: "Coward."*

**A mysterious feminine voice speaks from within the gilded sarcophagus as Zalthir pries it open, offering help to the party while the wraith lurks inside.**

*The same disembodied voice that first called out to the party upon entering the tomb now speaks from inside the wraith's own sarcophagus, where a magical sword rests on a mummified corpse.*

> "in the sarcophagus, I can help you."
> — The Voice

## Chapter 029

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/029-session-untitled.md`; chapter 29; section Memorable Moments

> "I've been waiting for somebody to save me and I get a monk."
> — Dawnbringer

*The sentient sword's indignant reaction on learning her rescuer is Zalthir, a monk who cannot even wield her*

> "Oh, God, not a talking magic sword."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's reaction to the voice from the sarcophagus. He later added that he wished she had been "a less squeaky, talking mace"*

> "You could have been like a glove."
> — Zalthir

*The monk's reply to a sword that wanted a fighter instead*

**Thorin, rather than attacking Brysis, reaches into the sarcophagus and claims the sentient sword Dawnbringer, who immediately manifests a blade of pure radiance like a lightsaber.**

*A dramatic pivot in the middle of combat as Thorin prioritizes claiming the legendary weapon over striking the enemy*

> "Dawnbringer here! Let me show you what I can do!"
> — Dawnbringer

*The sword's first words as its radiant blade springs into existence, followed by "Finally, light!"*

> "You just won the award for pissing off the demonic creature."
> — GM

*After Daz's three blind Scorching Rays land for 23 damage*

> "I have been waiting this millennium to be freed of this curse, and you will not defeat me."
> — Brysis

*Brysis's defiant declaration after being scorched by multiple rays of fire, moments before Gyrgum's lingering acid destroyed her*

> "We will defeat you with your own talking sword."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's answer to Brysis*

> "Wow, a sword that can read."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum marvels at Dawnbringer's literacy. The sword pauses and replies, "seriously?"*

> "Does anyone have an invisible key?"
> — Gyrgum

*The party is confronted with an invisible chest*

> "My punches are all driven by Dex. You don't want me rolling strength. That will not end well."
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir's plan to punch the decoy sarcophagus lid off runs into a Strength check, so Thorin shoves it instead*

**Sovereign Basidia reveals that a previous group of travelers accepted Phylo's offer, were taken to the Garden of Welcome, and were never seen or mentioned again.**

*A chilling warning delivered quietly during the tour, implying the Garden of Welcome is a trap or worse*

> "For Neverlight Grove is on the verge of something great, something marvelous. Celebrate as the day of joy is nigh."
> — Sovereign Phylo

*Phylo's ominous welcome to the "friendly softers"*

> "Yes, Basidia, you are my sovereign and co-equal in all things."
> — Sovereign Phylo

*Phylo agrees to stay behind while Basidia leads the tour. Gyrgum remarks on their "two-in-the-box style of management," and Zalthir's eyes squint at "very bad memories"*

**Basidia examines Sarith and announces she does not recognize the spores infecting him — remarkable, since myconid sovereigns can identify nearly every spore in the Underdark.**

*A quiet but alarming revelation suggesting the corruption affecting the grove originates from something entirely unknown and possibly demonic*

> "You know, in the community I come from, we like to say that there's no magical solutions to social problems."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's response to Basidia's account of Phylo's segregation of the circles*

> "What you don't want a zombie grick joining our team? That sounds so fun."
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir embraces the plan to reanimate the grick alpha as an ally*

> "I don't trust the dark. I've been in the dark for a very long time."
> — Dawnbringer

**Dawnbringer refuses to be deactivated at night, keeping Thorin and the entire party awake with her persistent, blazing radiance because she never wants to return to the dark.**

*The party discovers their powerful new weapon comes with a significant and inconvenient personality quirk*

**The party realizes the southern chamber is the fake tomb Fargas Rumblefoot warned them about — completely unguarded, its only curse already destroyed along with the wraith.**

*After bracing for another dangerous encounter, the party finds nothing but a decoy sarcophagus whose lid flies off with a light push. Gyrgum's verdict: "Oh, they didn't even bother to guard it."*

## Chapter 030

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/030-session-untitled.md`; chapter 30; section Memorable Moments

**Daz systematically freezes the swamp in a checkerboard pattern to play "Battleship" with a hidden Shambling Mound, announcing "Have I shot the battleship yet?" as he methodically freezes sections of moss.**

*Daz turns monster hunting into a board game, trying to flush out a camouflaged Shambling Mound with Shape Water.*

> "Unfortunately, cantrips don't work that way. Otherwise, you would have the mage hand could do heart attack spell."
> — GM

*The GM shuts down Daz's first plan to freeze the Shambling Mound's insides. Daz rereads the spell and finds a better angle: "It says you freeze the water, assuming there's no creatures in it. Oh, well, that's perfect."*

**Daz hurls a fireball blindly into Zalthir's magical darkness, detonating it right on top of the Grick Alpha without being able to see the target at all.**

*Unable to see the creature through the magical darkness, Daz trusts his memory of its location and lets the fireball fly. The Grick saves, and Daz grumbles: "That could have been 26." / "I'd have been better off with magic missile."*

> "I love it when Zalthir goes first of many actions because you know some crazy shit's about to happen."
> — Daz

*Zalthir interrupts the turn order after his first strike with "That was the first of many actions," then chains a Stunning Strike attempt and a flurry of extra attacks.*

**The Grick Alpha misses every single attack against Thorin and Zalthir due to the magical darkness, prompting the group to joke that the creature is drunk and pondering its life choices before it decides to flee.**

*The Grick Alpha fails all three of its attacks in a row, leaving the table in stitches. The GM narrates: "The creature is confused. It ponders its life for a moment." Gyrgum adds, "What choices it has made?" and the GM replies, "Well, one of those choices, it's decided to run."*

> "Should have fled faster."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's epitaph for the Grick Alpha, cut down by attacks of opportunity as it runs.*

> "One of the material components is a toothbrush, you know, for afterward."
> — Gyrgum

*The table riffs on the visual of Tasha's Caustic Brew as the orc cleric vomiting acid. Gyrgum admits he made the component up, and later "pull[s] out the toothbrush" after the Grick dies.*

**Gyrgum summons spectral platinum dragons that swirl around him, and combined with Zalthir's grapple locking the Shambling Mound in place, the holy aura repeatedly scorches the creature until it collapses into lifeless vines.**

*Gyrgum and Zalthir combine Spirit Guardians and grappling into a devastating trap that the Shambling Mound cannot escape. Gyrgum's reaction to the killing blow: "One of my spells was useful."*

> "Damn you, Gyrgum, do your thing."
> — Thorin

*Thorin bursts free of the Shambling Mound's engulfing mass, still muttering about having smelt better cologne, and the spirit guardians finish the job.*

> "Nobody's going to get steam damage for me melting the lake, are they?"
> — Daz

*Daz, reading the fireball's radius, does a quick safety check before detonating it next to the pool.*

> "That clarification terrifies me."
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir moves in to punch the Shambling Mound. The GM asks whether he is in the water with it, then insists that a GM asking a question "doesn't mean it necessarily means it's a bad decision."*

**Zalthir's player confuses the Grick Alpha's resistances with the Shambling Mound's, nearly talking Daz out of a fireball the Grick Alpha was not resistant to, prompting the table to joke about calling 911 for "confusing the metadata of two different monsters."**

*Mid-combat monster stat confusion leads to a near-tactical blunder and a round of laughter at the table. The GM asks, "Zalthir, you okay? Do we need to call the hospital?"*

> "Well, we have some that are tall and some that are big."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's first attempt to describe surface architecture to Gasbide, who promptly demands shapes, materials, windows, and doors.*

> "I dream of building a fungal tower even greater than Yggmorgus."
> — Gasbide

*The obsessive Myconid architect reveals his grand ambition to a bewildered party after grilling Gyrgum and Zalthir about surface buildings, before a long talk about construction with Daz.*

> "Oh, yes, it's called shade."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's answer when Gasbide asks for a construction that could protect him from the sun.*

**Gasbide cryptically mentions a coming "Great Awakening" and a celebration involving something called Araumycos, promised by Sovereign Phylo, though he has no idea what Araumycos actually is.**

*The party gets their first hint that something ominous is being planned at Neverlight Grove under Phylo's direction. In Gasbide's words: "Phylo said that there would be a great awakening, that everything will be better after the great celebration."*

## Chapter 031

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/031-session-untitled.md`; chapter 31; section Memorable Moments

**Sarith, a long-time companion of the party, suffers a horrific end as his head explodes in the Garden of Welcome, transforming him into a mindless drow spore servant who must then be cut down by his former allies.**

*A shocking and grim turning point in the battle, ending the mystery of Sarith's fate in the most gruesome way possible.*

> “Two things happen. One is the drow spore servant attacks Jimjar, and then Sarith's heads explodes. And he turns into a drow spore servant.”
> — DM

> “Please, for your gods of light, kill me.”
> — Xinaya

*A dying drow scout, buried to her neck and sprouting mushrooms, desperately begs the party she was sent to capture for a merciful death.*

> “We were sent out. Ilvara had sent us out to find you. Who would think that I would be begging you for my salvation?”
> — Xinaya

*Xinaya laughs hysterically at the reversal that has left her begging for salvation from the fugitives she was ordered to capture.*

**Daz and Zalthir are both infected with a creeping madness after Yestabrod releases a cloud of spores, with the DM secretly sending each player their unique affliction via private message.**

*The party is left uncertain about how the madness will manifest, adding a layer of tension and mystery going forward.*

> “But you have been inflicted with a madness.”
> — DM

> “So your worldview suddenly shifts. How about I put it that way?”
> — DM, to Zalthir

> “You're a fungus and you eat fungus. You eat fungi. You are a — just like I'm a mammal and I eat mammals.”
> — Thorin

*Thorin bluntly reduces myconid ecology to a familiar food-chain analogy while speaking with the Circle of Growers leader, Hebopbe.*

> “Well, I figured that out. Non-sentient ones don't talk to me nearly as much. Well, unless I've had a few mushrooms myself, but that's getting off topic.”
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum reassures Hebopbe that he understands the difference between a sentient myconid and an ordinary fungus, while making the explanation considerably less reassuring.*

**Zuggtmoy herself appears at the end of the session, leading a grotesque wedding procession of decaying, fungal humanoids chanting about her union with Araumycos, ending the session on a cliffhanger.**

*The Demon Queen of Fungi makes her entrance as the party stands in the Garden of Welcome, surrounded by the moaning victims of her corruption.*

> “Are we wedding crashers or are we wedding gifts?”
> — Thorin

> “You're not the wedding guest, you're the wedding feast.”
> — Gyrgum

*The party considers its likely role in Zuggtmoy's approaching ceremony.*

**Thorin's new sentient sword, Dawnbringer, is revealed to have an anxiety disorder about the dark — a significant problem given that his party member Zalthir regularly conjures magical darkness.**

*Thorin's player returns to the campaign to discover his character is now bonded to a legendary sword with a panic disorder, setting up inevitable comic chaos.*

> “So you got me a badass sword.”
> — Thorin

> “Well, actually, we got you a badass sword with personality and an anxiety disorder.”
> — DM

> “I mean, no, we're bonded at this point.”
> — Thorin

*After learning that Dawnbringer fears abandonment and may resent being replaced, Thorin accepts that the relationship has already become permanent.*

> “I like the idea of the comic relief of the sword having a, you know, Thorin charging into the darkness, and all of a sudden, the sword going, no, like we were stopping right at the edge of the darkness.”
> — DM

> “You must warn the other Drow of the presence of the demon queen of the fungi in the Underdark.”
> — Xinaya

*A dying drow scout, moments before her merciful death, urges Daz to warn their people about Zuggtmoy's presence in the Underdark.*

> “I mean, you know, I didn't ask for this marriage, but, you know, I'm going to make my vow.”
> — Thorin

*Thorin consults Dawnbringer before granting Xinaya a merciful death and characterizes their new bond as an arranged marriage he nevertheless intends to honor.*

## Chapter 032

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/032-session-untitled.md`; chapter 32; section Memorable Moments

**The party pieces together what Zuggtmoy intends to do with Araumycos.**

> “And now it dawns on you that Zuggtmoy is planning to mate with that fungal growth. Lord knows what’s going to happen as a result of that.”
> — DM

*The revelation transforms an apparently bizarre fungal parade into a demonic ritual involving two entities of staggering power.*

> “You’re saying this is not the wedding. It’s just a tribute.”
> — Thorin

*Thorin distills the party’s discovery into a Tenacious D reference: this is not the wedding itself, merely its rehearsal.*

**The party realizes exactly how far beyond them Zuggtmoy is.**

> “If this thing wishes to kill you, we will be rolling for new characters.”
> — DM

*The Demon Queen of Fungi is not a combat encounter the party can reasonably survive.*

> “So we don’t want to fight Zuggy.”
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum summarizes the tactical consensus with admirable economy.*

**The party experiences a shared vision of Zuggtmoy in her fungal tower, a towering figure made entirely of mold being dressed in a mycelium bridal gown by her fungal bridesmaids.**

> “They croon a strange, soothing song as they weave the substance of the giant figure into delicate lichen veils in a long mycelium train, like a bridal, like a bridal gown.”
> — DM

*A deeply unsettling revelation of the Demon Queen’s power and intent replaces the mock wedding tableau before their eyes.*

**Daz succumbs to vivid hallucinations produced by the madness around the Majestic Mushroom.**

> “Happy elf families, dwarves, dwarves and elves living together in peace and harmony.”
> — DM

> “A drow city with no spiders.”
> — DM

*To a drow accustomed to spider-covered cities, these peaceful and spiderless visions are nightmares. Daz responds by screaming, hiding, and trying to reject what he sees.*

**Zalthir, maddened by fungal spores, hoards the party’s discovered scrolls and refuses to share them.**

> “These are mine. I found them.”
> — Zalthir

> “I’ll work it out later. These are mine. Don’t touch them.”
> — Zalthir

*Spore-induced greed transforms the normally composed Zalthir into a possessive hoarder, alarming his companions.*

> “I don’t know what came over me, but I just needed to have this stuff. It was really critical. I apologize. It was bizarre.”
> — Zalthir

*After Thorin cures him, Zalthir can offer no explanation beyond the overwhelming compulsion he felt.*

> “I am a ghost. I am a traitor. I am a gem cutter. I am not a doctor.”
> — Pelek

*Asked to diagnose Zalthir’s behavior, the ghost firmly explains the limits of his professional expertise.*

> “It’s a bad scene. We should get out of here.”
> — Thorin

*Thorin gives Basidia and Rasharoo the concise version of the party’s report.*

> “Bros before toadstools.”
> — Thorin

*The party decides that rescuing Stool and Rumpadump takes priority before they flee Neverlight Grove.*

> “What part of stealthy do you people not understand?”
> — Zalthir, as voiced by the DM

*The party’s attempt to hide in plain sight in front of Phylo is undermined by Gyrgum signaling danger and Dawnbringer illuminating the myconids.*

> “If you want to live, come with me.”
> — Rasharoo

*As the grove turns on Basidia and its trees begin to move, Rasharoo commits to immediate evacuation.*

**Zalthir physically grabs Basidia and drags her away from a suicidal charge at the corrupted Phylo, saving her life as the grove’s trees animate around them.**

> “Well, mechanically, I do that through punching.”
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir applies his usual skill set to an improvised rescue, stopping Basidia just before she throws her life away.*

> “It will all be well. The Neverlight Grove will be better than ever. And the great cedar will make us all so happy. Basidia, don’t go. Stay.”
> — Phylo

*The fully corrupted Phylo calls after the fleeing party and Basidia as the grove descends into madness and violence.*

> “Would you like to bet?”
> — Jimjar

*Immediately after claiming that he knows exactly how to reach Blingdenstone, Jimjar naturally tries to make a wager out of it.*

**Gyrgum delivers the killing blow to the rocktopus with a mace strike after chanting a brief invocation.**

> “The octopus slayer. I exalt.”
> — Gyrgum

*The party had whittled down the creature on a narrow ledge above a gas-filled pit; Gyrgum’s finishing blow ends the threat and earns him a new title.*

> “My characters may not always be powerful, but they’re always proud.”
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum explains the philosophy behind celebrating the rocktopus victory.*

**Thorin and Gyrgum respond to being trapped by immediately professionalizing the party’s maps.**

> “So I take out my cartographer tools and start mapping as we go just to be.”
> — Thorin

> “And I use my calligrapher’s supplies to make sure that it was well labeled and in a tasteful font.”
> — Gyrgum

*If the party must be lost in an underground maze, they will at least be lost with impeccable documentation.*

> “That’s weak, man. That’s like really weak.”
> — Spiderbait

*Even Jimjar’s fellow travelers reject his convoluted attempt to distract everyone with a poetic bet.*

> “It’s not my best work.”
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar concedes that his prophecy-like wager about Zalthir’s next emotion is failing to land.*

> “I just, I was really trying to be like a good guide, but like, this is just awkward.”
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar sheepishly admits he got the party lost after his increasingly cryptic bets fail to distract anyone.*

## Chapter 040

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/040-session-crystals-ghosts-and-crazed-earth.md`; chapter 40; section Memorable Moments

> “Would I be the first gelatinous cube ghost?”
> — Glabbagool

*Glabbagool’s earnest response after Burrow Warden Jadger explained that dead adventurers could still join the coming battle as ghosts.*

> “What is necessity?”
> — Diggermattock Hall attendee

*The meeting’s philosophical response when the party questioned whether all the proposed side quests were truly necessary.*

> “But just for the record, at no point in time will the source of this booze be ascribed to my tavern and ruin the Foaming Mug’s good name.”
> — Tappy Foamstrap

*Tappy’s condition for supplying old or spoiled alcohol to weaponize against oozes.*

> “Well, only if you don’t want to be the hero who saved the city.”
> — Party member

*The party immediately reframed Tappy’s questionable booze as an advertising opportunity.*

**Glabbagool gets drunk from cleaning spilled alcohol off the tavern floor and starts singing gelatinous cube sea shanties.**

*The intelligent gelatinous cube becomes unusually loopy and musical, teaching the party that alcohol may affect other gelatinous creatures.*

> “We have now learned we can make gelatinous cubes drunk with alcohol, which may be handy for our upcoming battle.”
> — Party member

*The party turns Glabbagool’s impromptu tavern performance into a tactical discovery.*

> “It’s like sunglasses that keep the Medusa from—”
> — Party member

*The group finds a practical way to understand the Rockblight crystals’ protection from harmful gaze attacks.*

**Thorin rolls a natural 20 on Arcana; combined with Daz’s expertise, the result reveals that the crystals protect against gaze attacks when creatures are viewed through them.**

*The discovery explains why the petrified deep gnome had raised a crystal toward his face and gives the party a potential defense against whatever lies deeper in the Rockblight.*

> “They’re coming! Everyone must hide!”
> — Udhask

*The frightened ghost shouts a warning after being punched by Zalthir.*

> “I want to non-lethally punch the ghost.”
> — Zalthir

*The party attempts to preserve an already-dead opponent long enough to learn where it will flee.*

> “Well, then I bet a ghost that’s been hit with a mace will be even more frightened.”
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum applies practical adventuring logic after remembering that a frightened ghost might retreat to its lair and treasure.*

> “When something strange in the neighborhood.”
>
> “Who are you going to call?”
>
> “Ghost cleaners.”
> — Glabbagool and the party

*Collecting Udhask’s bones naturally inspires an imperfect Underdark rendition of “Ghostbusters.”*

> “Is this a bird?”
> — Glabbagool

*The cube’s confused reaction when the winged stone figures suddenly animate.*

**The gargoyles’ carefully prepared ambush is derailed by the appearance of a friendly gelatinous cube with googly eyes.**

*Even creatures with a standard “pretend to be a statue” strategy were unprepared for Glabbagool.*

> “You have to get checked out for rabies after this.”
> — Party member

*Gyrgum is bitten and clawed by a gargoyle for ten points of damage.*

**A crazed earth elemental erupts from the ground as the gargoyle battle rages, ending the session on a cliffhanger.**

*The hostile elemental’s arrival confirms that the corruption affecting summoned earth elementals is real.*

> “I wonder what happens if I use Maximilian’s Earthen Grasp on an Earth Elemental.”
> — Party member

*The session ends with an unresolved question about using earth-manipulating magic against living earth.*

*Revived at 1 hit point after the Medusa falls. Zalthir normally fights in his own magical darkness and had given it up so the ice mirror would work*

*On entering the menhir chamber*

*As the first Earth Elemental erupts from the wall*

*After the party coordinates to attack simultaneously, Zalthir delivers a crushing blow*

*After Bimble's ramblings about the Lady of Rot, the party realizes a major demonic threat has emerged*

## Chapter 044

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/044-session-from-zuggtmoy-to-entemoch-s-boon.md`; chapter 44; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir successfully executes a Shadow Step to redirect a catastrophic magical explosion through the void, safely breaching the time-snagged wall.**

*After meditating and channeling frustration into the void, Zalthir performs a precision acrobatic maneuver as gnome explosives detonate*

- Zalthir: "I mean, I've been sitting here this whole time meditating on the nature of this challenge and in turn, the void, trying to channel all my frustration about the gardening back into the void, right?"
- DM: "I like it. Well done. Let's give you a plus two and advantage on that."
- DM: "The gnomes are all high-fiving themselves, saying how they set up the explosion just right to break the wall. You know, they're ignoring the fact that you might be smithereens right now."

**The Shambling Mound miraculously rolls two natural 20s in a row to resist both Maximilian's Earthen Grasp and Tasha's Caustic Brew.**

*Daz and Gyrgum's spells completely fail as the creature defies all odds with consecutive critical saves*

- DM, after the second save: "I rolled a natural 20 for the second time."
- Shambling Mound: "with my last breath. I spit on thee."
- Glabbagool to Daz: "I noticed that you had these spells that never missed. Why didn't you use those?" Then he taps Daz with a pseudopod: "don't worry, you'll get better next time."
- Daz: "Yeah, I'm just having a bad day, Glabbagool."

**Zuggtmoy psychically contacts the party, revealing her plan to use oozes to 'soften the shell' so her garden may 'take root in the flesh beneath'.**

*Just as the party finishes harvesting lichen, they are pulled into a psychic conference call between Zuggtmoy and the Pudding King*

- Zuggtmoy: "the ooze is crude, my little king, but I will allow your Lord to soften the shell so that my garden may take root in the flesh beneath. Digest them all. I will claim this outpost."
- Gyrgum: "Do psychic conference calls include video or is it audio only?"
- DM: "This is an audio only one."

**Daz solves the Galeb Duhr puzzle by arranging resonant geodes and humming to complete the ancient 'handshake protocol'.**

*After rolling a 21 on Arcana, Daz successfully mimics the linguistic pattern the stone sentinels were waiting for*

- DM: "You get the impression that the stones are singing the first half of a duet. They cast their voice into the dark and when the dark does not answer, they lock their joints and wait to ask again."
- DM: "They are paralyzed by the silence."

**The Shambling Mound grapple as hockey game** (additional)

- DM: "It's like a hockey game... There's lots of clutching and grabbing and shoving and maybe a little bit of fist punches into the chest where the chest protector is so nobody gets hurt accidentally."

**Daz negotiates with Dasco Pickshine** (additional)

- Daz: "Yeah, we can put everything back like we found it and just let everything go to shit again."
- Daz: "So like go bring out some token of appreciation for us. And it's not a large enough token, we'll tell you to bring a second token and then we'll be on our way."

**Clockwork basilisk** (additional)

- Gyrgum: "Clockwork basilisk. That's going to be the name of my new DD band."
- Later, as one basilisk panics, Gyrgum: "You know, eventually my punk band will break up, and one of the new forks of it will be a frantic basilisk."

**Thorin objects to elemental labor.**

> "Okay, so I'm uncomfortable with subjugating the Earth Elementals and putting them into hard labor. This is offensive to me."
> — Thorin

> "I'm just saying it's like, you know, I feel like they're going to do us a solid, no pun intended. And we're going to repay them by putting them to work."
> — Thorin

> "So, I mean, when the revolution comes, I want to be one of the good ones."
> — Thorin

> "You know, we obtain this for defense, not, you know, not so that you could create a two-tiered economy with a caste system."
> — Thorin

> "So I think we start a union."
> — Thorin

> "Should we just give them all socks?"
> — Gyrgum

**Thorin versus Chipgrin.**

> "I mean, aren't gnomes supposed to have like awesome inventions and be like all steampunky and shit?"
> — Thorin
> "Those are not deep gnomes."
> — Chipgrin (GM)
> "Oh, so deep means backwards and enslavers. Gotcha."
> — Thorin's player

**The party rolls three natural 20s on persuasion checks while arguing against elemental slavery, completely swaying Blingdenstone's leadership.**

*During the heated debate about elemental labor ethics, the party's arguments were so compelling that they disrupted the entire economy*

> "Oh, oh, three out of four nat 20s."
> — GM

> "Yes, we've now officially disrupted the overall economy of Blingdenstone, which is now, you know, you've now become the toasts of the town."
> — GM

> "Are you really trying to become like the Drow?"
> — GM, framing Daz's argument

*Daz makes a provocative comparison between using elemental labor and Menzoberranzan's slave society*

> "Like, have you ever heard of dragons trying to enslave everybody? All the time, but I'm going to leave out those bits."
> — Gyrgum's player, on building his Bahamut argument

> "My monkish ways don't want to take advantage of this poor plain creature. I don't even know what these are, but whatever it is, yes."
> — Zalthir

**The Ember Vanguard's reputation precedes them.**

> "Daz is now, you know, an equal of Elminster the sage."
> — GM

**The GM sings the battle montage.**

> "But I like the parody because it has my favorite one. It has, I like both of them, but God has smiled upon you this day, the fate of the nation in your hands."
> — GM

> "I apologize for my singing."
> — GM

**Everybody in Blingdenstone is about the margin.**

> "You're beginning to sense everybody in Blingdenstone is about the margin."
> — GM
> "It sure seems like it. It's a very mercantile kind of a place."
> — Gyrgum

**Thorin accidentally inspires Valimor with modern marketing concepts like product numbering and platform models, opening his eyes to late-stage capitalism.**

*During scabbard shopping, casual suggestions about the 'Vaultmaster 9000' lead to an excited discussion about product tiers and replaceable components*

> "How much for the Vaultmaster 9000?"
> — Thorin
> "This is not the Vaultmaster 9000. This is the Vaultmaster's locking scabbard."
> — Valimor Brightgem (GM)

> "Well, and the thing is, you want to give them big numbers with like thousands because then, you know, it gives you space to introduce intermediate models."
> — Gyrgum

> "Well, you know, Thorin, what you just told me has opened my eyes to the possibility of marketing and financial management that I had not considered."
> — Valimor Brightgem (GM)

> "It's more like late-stage capitalism plus one."
> — Thorin

> "Dawnbringer's all like gender as a social construct."
> — Thorin's player

**Gnome Labor, not Summoned Labor!**

*Senni's chant that roused the people against using summoned elemental labor*

## Chapter 046

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/046-session-zalthir-s-daring-grapple.md`; chapter 46; section Memorable Moments

> "I'll bet 10 gold Daz's reflection is better looking than the real thing."
> — Chipgrin

*Joking about the psychic slime mirrors before the party enters the dangerous corridor*

> "I don't need to look at my reflection. We got a mission. I'm not playing parlor games."
> — Daz

*Chipgrin's reply: "now that explains why this man should be the general."*

> "There will be no dwarf tossing"
> — Thorin

*Declaring his position when the party discusses having the Earth Elemental throw party members. Zalthir answers: "Don't worry. We're good. Only dragonborns are being tossed here."*

> "Do not gloat. We still plot."
> — Gyrgum

*Said as the Pudding King monologues that Blingdenstone will be turned into "Blingdenpuddle"*

**Zalthir is thrown 30 feet by an Earth Elemental, teleports another 60 feet, closes the rest of the distance on foot, and grapples the Pudding King with tentacles, dragging him away from his allies**

*The climactic assault on the Pudding King, executing a daring plan to isolate the villain. The GM: "that was a good tactical move, getting him away from his allies. I think that deserves some inspiration."*

> "I would never hurt my friends... But there was that one time very early on when we went to the big pool of slimes where I heard a voice calling me"
> — Glabbagool

*Expressing loyalty while revealing potential vulnerability to the Pudding King's influence*

**The Pudding King transforms into an ooze form when grappled, allowing him to automatically hit Zalthir without rolling**

*The villain reveals a dangerous defensive ability at the end of the session.*

**Dawnbringer is upset that Thorin would take a jump that might land them both in a pool of acid.**

*Jimjar bets Thorin that he can't cross the pit with his pack. Thorin is ready to jump. Gyrgum is worried and notices that the bet didn't say jump, and so Thorin can just walk across.*

> "I'll bet five gold Thorin can't clear that gap with his pack on. Any takers?"
> — Jimjar

> "well, look how easy it is. There's nothing to it at all."
> — Gyrgum

*Talking Thorin into walking the bridge instead of jumping the pit*

> "Yes, it's tea from my mines, but provided by the Pickshine Mines."
> — Dasco Pickshine

*Explaining the branded tea. Asked what kind of tea he mines: "I don't mind tea." Gyrgum: "It's a branding exercise. It's not a digging exercise."*

> "There will be no, there will be no picking on the unalived."
> — A Blingdenstone ghost

*Responding to Zalthir's suggestion to "send the ghosts. They're not going to die, right?"*

> "It isn't water. It's a massive, semi-sentient basin of black pudding waiting like a stomach for anything to fall in."
> — Wererat scout

> "That's a dissolved trap if I ever saw one. The bedrock's gone soft there, and that muck will eat through your boots before you can say toadstool."
> — Dasco Pickshine

> "There's some big brain slime up there singing a song that turns a man's mind into puddle."
> — Chipgrin

> "Can I call you general?" / "No, you can't call me general. You can call me Daz."
> — Dasco Pickshine and Daz

> "I don't like being the general." ... "I like just being Daz."
> — Daz

*Glabbagool pauses: "What's a general?"*

> "no, no, no, don't eat them slow, quickly. Eat them slowly. Ooze them slowly."
> — The Pudding King

*Directing his troops against the Blingdenstone army*

## Chapter 047

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/047-session-the-fall-of-the-pudding-king.md`; chapter 47; section Memorable Moments

> "I have shed my humanoid form for the supreme power! … I shall reduce all of gnome kind into nothing but ooze!"
> — Pudding King

*The Pudding King reveals his transformation into a sentient ooze, telepathically screaming his intentions*

> "Turn it down just a notch, Mr. Ooz."
> — Gyrgum

> "Yeah, we can hear you just fine."
> — Daz

> "I mean, we can fight and all, but we don't have to yell at each other."
> — Thorin

*The party's unimpressed response to the telepathic screaming*

> "What's the point of being a demon? What's the point of being allying yourself with a demonic power if you can't scream at people? … If I wanted to speak calmly and rationally, I would have made a deal with the devil."
> — Pudding King

> "Is the devil calm and rational?"
> — Gyrgum

> "Well, they're the ones who sign contracts all the time."
> — GM as Narrator

**Zalthir grapples the Pudding King in ooze form and drags him into Glabbagool, prompting Thorin to joke that the gelatinous cube must be thinking 'this tickles'**

*An absurd tactical maneuver during the heat of battle*

> "Glabbagool's like, this tickles."
> — Thorin

> "That's only slightly ominous, GM."
> — Zalthir, after the DM reminds him that Glabbagool was touched by demonic power last time

> "What kind of pudding is this guy anyway? As long as it's not tapioca."
> — Gyrgum, after being hit by the green slime

> "So if it's green, is that pistachio?"
> — Gyrgum

> "It's a toxic acidic ooze that rips through your armor and eats your flesh and consumes you and turns you into one with the Juiblex."
> — GM as Narrator

> "You should taste it, and it will eat you alive from the inside."
> — Pudding King

> "Why are you hurting me?"
> — Glabbagool (in Pudding King's body)

*The party realizes Glabbagool and the Pudding King have swapped bodies when a familiar voice comes from the wrong ooze*

> "I, we, we, we met, we met in a cake. That's where we've met."
> — Pudding King (in Glabbagool's body), answering Thorin's test question

> "Yeah, not so much against wizards. We're smart."
> — Daz, answering Prince Livid's taunt as the psychic blast lands ("This works so well against oozes." — GM as Prince Livid)

> "Behold the true feast, the soul's final blend!"
> — Pudding King

*The Pudding King's final telepathic scream as he dissolves and dies from Zalthir's attacks*

> "Nobody thinks to grapple the ooze."
> — GM as Narrator

> "I am a sentient ooze. I can control my viscosity. You wouldn't even need a cover on it. Plus, how would I see?"
> — Glabbagool

> "I don't know how you see now."
> — Gyrgum

> "It's uh, good, good to think outside the blob"
> — Gyrgum

## Chapter 048

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/048-session-the-knucklebone-of-truth.md`; chapter 48; section Memorable Moments

**Thorin, the least magically inclined member of the party, succeeds where all the spellcasters failed. He correctly identifies the psychic center of the Hall of Melting Mirrors using a lucky roll and dwarvish intuition.**

*Chipgrin remarks he should have asked the dwarf first, before awkwardly clarifying to Daz that he meant no offense by lumping him in with the 'surface dweller types'.*

> "You know, I should have asked the dwarf before I asked all of these surface dweller types."
> — Chipgrin

> "Look, look, it's been kind of a long day, been kind of busy. I'm a little off, but I'm very happy that Thorin was able to help us here because I'm just like, I need a rest."
> — Daz

> "What's the fun in that?"
> — A Gnomish miner, responding to Gyrgum's suggestion that they "just use a little bit of magic"

*The miner followed it with a tirade that ended in "a mend here and a mend there" and contraptions nobody understands.*

> "Thorin, Thorin, my friend, I think that you have become an honorary member of the crew."
> — Diggermattock

> "See, a bit of salt, a bit of sweat, and the house is back in order. You lot are much better at fixing things than at breaking them. Let's get out of here before I decide to bet my winnings on whether we hit daylight in the next hour."
> — Jimjar

> "Back in my day at IBM, we solved this problem using buffer caches."
> — A ghost at the ooze breach (the GM's deliberately anachronistic joke), while everyone rolls their eyes

> "And I was just able to tell them all, chill out, that it's a great world. We could all be friends."
> — Glabbagool

> "I don't even care whether I won the bet if the oozes disappeared."
> — Gyrgum

> "Sometimes the universe just drops a loaded die in your pocket and hopes you're smart enough to use it."
> — Jimjar

> "The old mule swore it was carved from the knucklebone of a saint who never told a lie, said it had a memory for the truth. I thought he was just trying to cover his losses."
> — Jimjar

*Explaining the origin of his mysterious bone die after using it to pacify an entire army of oozes through Glabbagool.*

> "So I just thought, how do you counteract demons that shouldn't be here because of some unbelievably unrandom event? Well, you roll a die of a saint."
> — Jimjar

> "So I figured I was either getting 10 platinum pieces or we're all dead. What is the downside in that?"
> — Jimjar

> "The Vanguard has given us some miracles, but miracles don't pay the guards."
> — Dorbo Diggermattock

> "If all of us come together, we can defeat the demons for good."
> — Gyrgum

> "Daz, you took absolute accountability while the tunnels were screaming. The Goldwhisker clan doesn't forget who stood in the light so we could fight in the dark."
> — Chief Chipgrin

> "House always wins, wizard. You settled the ledgers, gave the ghosts a home, and even got Dasco Pickshine to pay a fair wage for once. Clean win!"
> — Jimjar

**Thorin uses his Know Your Enemy ability to inspect Jimjar and discovers he possesses divine-level immunities and resistances. Jimjar notices, winks, and vanishes into thin air, hinting the party will meet again.**

*The party had long suspected Jimjar was more than he appeared; this confirmed it, with his stat block resembling that of an angel or deity.*

> "Those are suspiciously similar to the stat block for angels"
> — Zalthir

> "Juiblex was a loud, messy neighbor, but this sister, she prefers to move in while you're sleeping."
> — Jimjar

*Pointing ominously at a pale mushroom growing at impossible speed from a crack in the wall, foreshadowing Zuggtmoy's encroachment, just before casually walking away.*

## Chapter 049

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/049-session-a-sign-from-lolth-or-just-a-spider.md`; chapter 49; section Memorable Moments

> "He's a fun guy, right?"
> "That's why I said he's clearly a fun guy. So that's the reaction I want."
> — Thorin

*Thorin points out that the infected drow scout, covered in fungal veins and mushrooms, is literally a "fun-gi." The first attempt passed in silence, and the table only laughed once Thorin explained it.*

> "Ilvara had made promises to two powerful people in Menzoberranzan, and your disappearance was very embarrassing and catastrophic."
> — Valen

*A terrified, fungus-covered drow scout explains to the very people he was sent to hunt exactly why his boss is furious with them. Just before, when Daz guessed Ilvara was mad "Because we left," Valen shot back: "Damn, you sure think we're important."*

> "And all we get is a damn used token."
> — Zalthir

*Zalthir sums up weeks of partying with a deity. If he had known, he says, he "would have done all sorts of investigations on special abilities."*

> "Who walks into a room full of kuo-toa and expects to see Demogorgon show up, right? Am I right?"
> — Valen

*Valen recounts the misadventures of Ilvara's pursuit force.*

> "She's going to turn me into one of those sporish thingy-moblops. And I don't want to turn into a sporish thingy bugs."
> — Valen

> "Well, it sounds like you should come with us to the Overbright. It's either that or face Ilvara."
> — Gyrgum

> "the spider queen does not share her web with fungal filth and Ilvara is getting on my nerves"
> — Asha Vandree

*Asha scrapes fungus from her clothes, unaware that a spider familiar is watching.*

**Thorin tells Valen "it's clear that you're a fun guy so you know we're gonna help you out here," and Valen laughs the nervous laugh from the Sopranos scene where everyone laughs at the mob boss's bad joke.**

**Daz conjures dancing lights into a divine, Lolth-like form above the spider familiar, making Asha Vandree believe she is receiving a sacred vision from the goddess Lolth herself.**

*A bold act of magical deception from within the enemy camp: convincing a fanatical drow priestess, already at odds with her leader, that the spider was an avatar of Lolth. It earned Daz an Inspiration point.*
